using System.Security.Claims;
using Microsoft.AspNetCore.Authentication;
using WinCapture.Models;

namespace WinCapture.Services;

public sealed class MicrosoftIdentityClaimsTransformation(
    MicrosoftUserService microsoftUserService) : IClaimsTransformation
{
    // Maps the authenticated Microsoft Entra account to a WinCapture user and adds the required claims.
    public async Task<ClaimsPrincipal> TransformAsync(ClaimsPrincipal principal)
    {
        if (!principal.Identity?.IsAuthenticated ?? true)
        {
            return principal;
        }

        var identity = principal.Identities
            .FirstOrDefault(identity => identity.IsAuthenticated);

        if (identity is null)
        {
            return principal;
        }

        var existingUserId = principal.FindFirst("wincapture_user_id")?.Value;

        if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(existingUserId))
        {
            return principal;
        }

        var objectId = principal.FindFirst("oid")?.Value;
        var email = principal.FindFirst("preferred_username")?.Value
            ?? principal.FindFirst("email")?.Value
            ?? principal.FindFirst("upn")?.Value;

        if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(objectId) || string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(email))
        {
            return principal;
        }

        var role = GetRole(principal);

        if (role is null)
        {
            return principal;
        }

        var name = principal.FindFirst("name")?.Value
            ?? principal.FindFirst("given_name")?.Value
            ?? email.Split('@')[0];

        var user = await microsoftUserService.GetOrCreateAsync(
            name,
            email.Trim().ToLowerInvariant(),
            objectId,
            role.Value);

        RemoveClaim(identity, "wincapture_user_id");
        RemoveClaim(identity, "authentication_type");

        identity.AddClaim(new Claim("wincapture_user_id", user.Id.ToString()));
        identity.AddClaim(new Claim("authentication_type", "Microsoft Entra ID"));

        return principal;
    }

    // Maps the Microsoft Entra app role to the corresponding WinCapture user role.
    private static UserRole? GetRole(ClaimsPrincipal principal)
    {
        var roles = principal.FindAll("roles")
            .Select(claim => claim.Value)
            .Where(role =>
                string.Equals(role, "WinCapture.Admin", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) ||
                string.Equals(role, "WinCapture.User", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
            .Distinct(StringComparer.OrdinalIgnoreCase)
            .ToArray();

        if (roles.Length != 1)
        {
            return null;
        }

        return string.Equals(roles[0], "WinCapture.Admin", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase)
            ? UserRole.Admin
            : UserRole.User;
    }

    // Removes existing claims of the specified type before adding updated values.
    private static void RemoveClaim(ClaimsIdentity identity, string claimType)
    {
        foreach (var claim in identity.FindAll(claimType).ToList())
        {
            identity.RemoveClaim(claim);
        }
    }
}

FILE: C:\Users\ShodhanVemulapalli\Downloads\WinCapture\backend\Services\MicrosoftUserService.cs
============================================================

using Microsoft.EntityFrameworkCore;
using WinCapture.Exceptions;
using WinCapture.Models;
using WinCapture.Repositories;

namespace WinCapture.Services;

public sealed class MicrosoftUserService(IUserRepository userRepository)
{
    // Validates the Microsoft account details and creates or updates the local user.
    public async Task<User> GetOrCreateAsync(
        string name,
        string email,
        string entraObjectId,
        UserRole role)
    {
        var normalizedEmail = email.Trim().ToLowerInvariant();
        var normalizedObjectId = entraObjectId.Trim();

        if (!IsValidEmail(normalizedEmail))
        {
            throw new UnauthorizedException(
                "The Microsoft account email address is invalid.");
        }

        if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(normalizedObjectId))
        {
            throw new UnauthorizedException(
                "The Microsoft account object ID is missing.");
        }

        if (role is not UserRole.User and not UserRole.Admin)
        {
            throw new UnauthorizedException(
                "The Microsoft account does not have a supported WinCapture app role.");
        }

        var normalizedName = string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(name)
            ? normalizedEmail.Split('@')[0]
            : name.Trim();

        if (normalizedName.Length > 200)
        {
            normalizedName = normalizedName[..200];
        }

        // Uses the Entra object ID as the stable identity key for the account.
        var user = await userRepository.GetByEntraObjectIdAsync(normalizedObjectId);

        if (user is null)
        {
            user = new User
            {
                Name = normalizedName,
                Email = normalizedEmail,
                EntraObjectId = normalizedObjectId,
                Role = role,
                CreatedAt = DateTime.UtcNow
            };

            try
            {
                await userRepository.AddAsync(user);
            }
            catch (DbUpdateException exception)
            {
                // A concurrent first sign-in may have inserted this OID already. Never fall back to email.
                var concurrentUser = await userRepository.GetByEntraObjectIdAsync(normalizedObjectId);
                if (concurrentUser is not null)
                {
                    return concurrentUser;
                }

                if (IsDuplicateKeyException(exception))
                {
                    throw new UnauthorizedException(
                        "The Microsoft account conflicts with an existing WinCapture profile.");
                }

                throw;
            }

            return user;
        }

        // Keeps the local user details and role in sync with Microsoft Entra.
        var changed = false;

        if (!string.Equals(
                user.Email,
                normalizedEmail,
                StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
        {
            user.Email = normalizedEmail;
            changed = true;
        }

        if (!string.Equals(
                user.Name,
                normalizedName,
                StringComparison.Ordinal))
        {
            user.Name = normalizedName;
            changed = true;
        }

        // Uses the Entra application role as the source of truth for the user's role.
        if (user.Role != role)
        {
            user.Role = role;
            changed = true;
        }

        if (changed)
        {
            await userRepository.UpdateAsync(user);
        }

        return user;
    }

    // Checks whether the supplied value is a valid email address.
    private static bool IsValidEmail(string email)
    {
        try
        {
            var address = new System.Net.Mail.MailAddress(email);

            return string.Equals(
                address.Address,
                email,
                StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase);
        }
        catch (FormatException)
        {
            return false;
        }
    }

    // Identifies SQL Server duplicate-key errors that can occur during concurrent account creation.
    private static bool IsDuplicateKeyException(DbUpdateException exception)
    {
        return exception.InnerException is
                   Microsoft.Data.SqlClient.SqlException sqlException &&
               (sqlException.Number == 2601 ||
                sqlException.Number == 2627);
    }
}


============================================================
FILE: C:\Users\ShodhanVemulapalli\Downloads\WinCapture\backend\Validators\FileValidator.cs
============================================================

using System;
using System.Buffers.Binary;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.IO;
using System.IO.Compression;
using System.Linq;
using System.Text;
using System.Threading.Tasks;
using System.Xml;
using System.Xml.Linq;
using Microsoft.AspNetCore.Http;
using WinCapture.Exceptions;

namespace WinCapture.Validators;

public sealed class FileValidator
{
    public const long MaximumFileSize = 10L * 1024L * 1024L;

    private const long MaximumExpandedArchiveSize = 64L * 1024L * 1024L;
    private const int MaximumArchiveEntries = 2048;
    private const int MaximumXmlCharacters = 1024 * 1024;
    private const int BufferSize = 81920;

    private static readonly IReadOnlyDictionary<string, string> AllowedContentTypes =
        new Dictionary<string, string>(StringComparer.OrdinalIgnoreCase)
        {
            [".jpg"] = "image/jpeg",
            [".jpeg"] = "image/jpeg",
            [".png"] = "image/png",
            [".webp"] = "image/webp",
            [".pdf"] = "application/pdf",
            [".txt"] = "text/plain",
            [".csv"] = "text/csv",
            [".docx"] = "application/vnd.openxmlformats-officedocument.wordprocessingml.document",
            [".xlsx"] = "application/vnd.openxmlformats-officedocument.spreadsheetml.sheet",
            [".pptx"] = "application/vnd.openxmlformats-officedocument.presentationml.presentation",
            [".mp4"] = "video/mp4",
            [".mov"] = "video/quicktime",
            [".webm"] = "video/webm",
            [".mp3"] = "audio/mpeg",
            [".wav"] = "audio/wav",
            [".m4a"] = "audio/mp4",
            [".zip"] = "application/zip"
        };

    private static readonly UTF8Encoding StrictUtf8 = new(false, true);
    private static readonly UTF8Encoding Utf8WithBom = new(true, true);
    private static readonly uint[] PngCrcTable = CreatePngCrcTable();

    // Checks the file size, extension, content type, and actual file structure before accepting an upload.
    public async Task ValidateAsync(IFormFile file)
    {
        if (file is null)
        {
            throw new BadRequestException("A file is required.");
        }

        if (file.Length == 0)
        {
            throw new BadRequestException("The uploaded file cannot be empty.");
        }

        if (file.Length > MaximumFileSize)
        {
            throw new PayloadTooLargeException(
                "The uploaded file exceeds the maximum allowed size of 10 MB.");
        }

        var extension = Path.GetExtension(file.FileName);

        if (!AllowedContentTypes.TryGetValue(extension, out var expectedContentType))
        {
            throw new BadRequestException("The uploaded file type is not supported.");
        }

        if (!string.Equals(
                file.ContentType,
                expectedContentType,
                StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
        {
            throw new BadRequestException("The uploaded file content type is invalid.");
        }

        var content = await ReadContentAsync(file);

        if (!HasValidContent(extension, content))
        {
            throw new BadRequestException(
                "The uploaded file content does not match its file type or is malformed.");
        }
    }

    // Reads the file into memory while enforcing the upload size limit and checking for incomplete input.
    private static async Task<byte[]> ReadContentAsync(IFormFile file)
    {
        await using var stream = file.OpenReadStream();
        using var buffer = new MemoryStream((int)file.Length);

        var chunk = new byte[BufferSize];
        long totalRead = 0;

        while (true)
        {
            var count = await stream.ReadAsync(chunk.AsMemory());

            if (count == 0)
            {
                break;
            }

            totalRead += count;

            if (totalRead > MaximumFileSize)
            {
                throw new PayloadTooLargeException(
                    "The uploaded file exceeds the maximum allowed size of 10 MB.");
            }

            await buffer.WriteAsync(chunk.AsMemory(0, count));
        }

        if (totalRead != file.Length)
        {
            throw new BadRequestException(
                "The uploaded file is incomplete or malformed.");
        }

        return buffer.ToArray();
    }

    // Selects the content validator that matches the uploaded file extension.
    private static bool HasValidContent(string extension, byte[] content) =>
        extension.ToLowerInvariant() switch
        {
            ".jpg" or ".jpeg" => IsValidJpeg(content),
            ".png" => IsValidPng(content),
            ".webp" => IsValidWebP(content),
            ".pdf" => IsValidPdf(content),
            ".txt" => IsValidText(content),
            ".csv" => IsValidCsv(content),
            ".docx" => IsValidOfficePackage(
                content,
                "word/document.xml",
                "application/vnd.openxmlformats-officedocument.wordprocessingml.document.main+xml"),
            ".xlsx" => IsValidOfficePackage(
                content,
                "xl/workbook.xml",
                "application/vnd.openxmlformats-officedocument.spreadsheetml.sheet.main+xml"),
            ".pptx" => IsValidOfficePackage(
                content,
                "ppt/presentation.xml",
                "application/vnd.openxmlformats-officedocument.presentationml.presentation.main+xml"),
            ".mp4" => IsValidIsoBmff(content, "mp4"),
            ".mov" => IsValidIsoBmff(content, "mov"),
            ".webm" => IsValidWebM(content),
            ".mp3" => IsValidMp3(content),
            ".wav" => IsValidWav(content),
            ".m4a" => IsValidIsoBmff(content, "m4a"),
            ".zip" => IsValidZip(content),
            _ => false
        };

    // Checks JPEG markers and walks image scan data until the end marker or another valid segment is found.
    private static bool IsValidJpeg(ReadOnlySpan<byte> content)
    {
        if (content.Length < 12 ||
            content[0] != 0xFF || content[1] != 0xD8 ||
            content[^2] != 0xFF || content[^1] != 0xD9)
        {
            return false;
        }

        var offset = 2;
        var hasFrame = false;
        var hasScan = false;
        var hasScanData = false;

        while (offset < content.Length)
        {
            if (content[offset] != 0xFF)
            {
                return false;
            }

            while (offset < content.Length && content[offset] == 0xFF)
            {
                offset++;
            }

            if (offset >= content.Length)
            {
                return false;
            }

            var marker = content[offset++];

            if (marker == 0xD9)
            {
                return hasFrame && hasScan && hasScanData && offset == content.Length;
            }

            // These markers do not contain a length field; a second SOI marker is not valid here.
            if (marker == 0xD8)
            {
                return false;
            }

            if (marker == 0x01)
            {
                continue;
            }

            if (marker is >= 0xD0 and <= 0xD7)
            {
                return false;
            }

            if (offset > content.Length - 2)
            {
                return false;
            }

            var segmentLength = BinaryPrimitives.ReadUInt16BigEndian(content[offset..]);

            if (segmentLength < 2 || segmentLength > content.Length - offset)
            {
                return false;
            }

            var segmentEnd = offset + segmentLength;

            if (marker is >= 0xC0 and <= 0xCF && marker is not 0xC4 and not 0xC8 and not 0xCC)
            {
                if (segmentLength < 11)
                {
                    return false;
                }

                var frameData = content.Slice(offset + 2, segmentLength - 2);
                var height = BinaryPrimitives.ReadUInt16BigEndian(frameData[1..]);
                var width = BinaryPrimitives.ReadUInt16BigEndian(frameData[3..]);
                var componentCount = frameData[5];

                if (width == 0 || height == 0 || componentCount == 0 ||
                    segmentLength != 8 + 3 * componentCount)
                {
                    return false;
                }

                hasFrame = true;
            }

            offset = segmentEnd;

            if (marker != 0xDA)
            {
                continue;
            }

            if (!hasFrame || segmentLength < 8)
            {
                return false;
            }

            var scanData = content.Slice(offset - segmentLength + 2, segmentLength - 2);
            var scanComponentCount = scanData[0];

            if (scanComponentCount == 0 || segmentLength != 6 + 2 * scanComponentCount)
            {
                return false;
            }

            hasScan = true;
            hasScanData = false;

            // Within scan data, FF 00 is a stuffed byte and D0-D7 are restart markers.
            while (offset < content.Length)
            {
                if (content[offset] != 0xFF)
                {
                    hasScanData = true;
                    offset++;
                    continue;
                }

                var markerStart = offset++;

                while (offset < content.Length && content[offset] == 0xFF)
                {
                    offset++;
                }

                if (offset >= content.Length)
                {
                    return false;
                }

                var scanMarker = content[offset];

                if (scanMarker == 0x00 || scanMarker is >= 0xD0 and <= 0xD7)
                {
                    hasScanData = true;
                    offset++;
                    continue;
                }

                if (!hasScanData)
                {
                    return false;
                }

                // Let the outer loop process the next marker, including EOI or another scan segment.
                offset = markerStart;
                break;
            }
        }

        return false;
    }

    // Checks PNG chunk lengths, CRC values, chunk ordering, and required image data.
    private static bool IsValidPng(ReadOnlySpan<byte> content)
    {
        ReadOnlySpan<byte> signature = [137, 80, 78, 71, 13, 10, 26, 10];

        if (content.Length < 45 || !content.StartsWith(signature))
        {
            return false;
        }

        var offset = 8;
        var sawHeader = false;
        var sawPalette = false;
        var sawImageData = false;
        var imageDataEnded = false;
        var sawEnd = false;
        byte colorType = 0;
        byte bitDepth = 0;

        while (offset <= content.Length - 12)
        {
            var length = BinaryPrimitives.ReadUInt32BigEndian(content[offset..]);

            if (length > int.MaxValue || length > content.Length - offset - 12)
            {
                return false;
            }

            var dataLength = (int)length;
            var type = content.Slice(offset + 4, 4);

            if (!IsValidPngChunkType(type))
            {
                return false;
            }

            var data = content.Slice(offset + 8, dataLength);
            var expectedCrc = BinaryPrimitives.ReadUInt32BigEndian(
                content.Slice(offset + 8 + dataLength, 4));

            if (ComputePngCrc(type, data) != expectedCrc)
            {
                return false;
            }

            if (!sawHeader)
            {
                if (!type.SequenceEqual("IHDR"u8) || dataLength != 13)
                {
                    return false;
                }

                var width = BinaryPrimitives.ReadUInt32BigEndian(data);
                var height = BinaryPrimitives.ReadUInt32BigEndian(data[4..]);
                bitDepth = data[8];
                colorType = data[9];

                if (width == 0 || height == 0 ||
                    data[10] != 0 || data[11] != 0 || data[12] > 1 ||
                    !IsValidPngBitDepth(colorType, bitDepth))
                {
                    return false;
                }

                sawHeader = true;
            }
            else if (type.SequenceEqual("IHDR"u8))
            {
                return false;
            }

            if (type.SequenceEqual("PLTE"u8))
            {
                if (sawImageData || sawPalette || dataLength == 0 || dataLength % 3 != 0 || dataLength > 768 ||
                    colorType is 0 or 4 ||
                    colorType == 3 && dataLength / 3 > (1 << bitDepth))
                {
                    return false;
                }

                sawPalette = true;
            }

            if (type.SequenceEqual("IDAT"u8))
            {
                if (imageDataEnded)
                {
                    return false;
                }

                sawImageData = true;
            }
            else if (sawImageData && !type.SequenceEqual("IEND"u8))
            {
                imageDataEnded = true;
            }

            // Unknown critical chunks cannot safely be ignored by this structural validator.
            if (!IsKnownPngChunk(type) && type[0] >= (byte)'A' && type[0] <= (byte)'Z')
            {
                return false;
            }

            offset += 12 + dataLength;

            if (type.SequenceEqual("IEND"u8))
            {
                sawEnd = dataLength == 0 && offset == content.Length;
                break;
            }
        }

        return sawHeader && sawImageData && sawEnd && (colorType != 3 || sawPalette);
    }

    // Rejects chunk names that do not follow the PNG chunk-type format.
    private static bool IsValidPngChunkType(ReadOnlySpan<byte> type)
    {
        if (type.Length != 4)
        {
            return false;
        }

        foreach (var value in type)
        {
            var isUppercase = value >= (byte)'A' && value <= (byte)'Z';
            var isLowercase = value >= (byte)'a' && value <= (byte)'z';

            if (!isUppercase && !isLowercase)
            {
                return false;
            }
        }

        return type[2] >= (byte)'A' && type[2] <= (byte)'Z';
    }

    // Recognizes PNG chunk types handled by this validator or defined as common ancillary chunks.
    private static bool IsKnownPngChunk(ReadOnlySpan<byte> type) =>
        type.SequenceEqual("IHDR"u8) ||
        type.SequenceEqual("PLTE"u8) ||
        type.SequenceEqual("IDAT"u8) ||
        type.SequenceEqual("IEND"u8) ||
        type.SequenceEqual("tRNS"u8) ||
        type.SequenceEqual("gAMA"u8) ||
        type.SequenceEqual("cHRM"u8) ||
        type.SequenceEqual("sRGB"u8) ||
        type.SequenceEqual("iCCP"u8) ||
        type.SequenceEqual("sBIT"u8) ||
        type.SequenceEqual("bKGD"u8) ||
        type.SequenceEqual("pHYs"u8) ||
        type.SequenceEqual("tEXt"u8) ||
        type.SequenceEqual("zTXt"u8) ||
        type.SequenceEqual("iTXt"u8) ||
        type.SequenceEqual("eXIf"u8) ||
        type.SequenceEqual("hIST"u8);

    // Checks whether the PNG bit depth is supported for the selected color type.
    private static bool IsValidPngBitDepth(byte colorType, byte bitDepth) =>
        colorType switch
        {
            0 => bitDepth is 1 or 2 or 4 or 8 or 16,
            2 => bitDepth is 8 or 16,
            3 => bitDepth is 1 or 2 or 4 or 8,
            4 or 6 => bitDepth is 8 or 16,
            _ => false
        };

    // Calculates the CRC used to verify PNG chunks.
    private static uint ComputePngCrc(ReadOnlySpan<byte> type, ReadOnlySpan<byte> data)
    {
        var crc = 0xFFFFFFFFu;

        foreach (var value in type)
        {
            crc = PngCrcTable[(int)((crc ^ value) & 0xFF)] ^ (crc >> 8);
        }

        foreach (var value in data)
        {
            crc = PngCrcTable[(int)((crc ^ value) & 0xFF)] ^ (crc >> 8);
        }

        return ~crc;
    }

    // Creates the lookup table used for PNG CRC calculations.
    private static uint[] CreatePngCrcTable()
    {
        var table = new uint[256];

        for (var index = 0; index < table.Length; index++)
        {
            var value = (uint)index;

            for (var bit = 0; bit < 8; bit++)
            {
                value = (value & 1) == 1
                    ? 0xEDB88320u ^ (value >> 1)
                    : value >> 1;
            }

            table[index] = value;
        }

        return table;
    }

    // Checks the WebP container size, chunk boundaries, and presence of an image chunk.
    private static bool IsValidWebP(ReadOnlySpan<byte> content)
    {
        if (content.Length < 20 ||
            !content[..4].SequenceEqual("RIFF"u8) ||
            !content.Slice(8, 4).SequenceEqual("WEBP"u8) ||
            BinaryPrimitives.ReadUInt32LittleEndian(content[4..]) != content.Length - 8)
        {
            return false;
        }

        var offset = 12;
        var hasImage = false;

        while (offset <= content.Length - 8)
        {
            var chunkLength = BinaryPrimitives.ReadUInt32LittleEndian(content[(offset + 4)..]);

            if (chunkLength > int.MaxValue || chunkLength > content.Length - offset - 8)
            {
                return false;
            }

            var chunkType = content.Slice(offset, 4);

            if ((chunkType.SequenceEqual("VP8 "u8) ||
                 chunkType.SequenceEqual("VP8L"u8)) && chunkLength > 0)
            {
                hasImage = true;
            }

            if (chunkType.SequenceEqual("VP8X"u8) && chunkLength != 10)
            {
                return false;
            }

            offset += 8 + (int)chunkLength + ((chunkLength & 1) == 1 ? 1 : 0);

            if (offset > content.Length)
            {
                return false;
            }
        }

        return offset == content.Length && hasImage;
    }

    // Checks the PDF header and requires an end-of-file marker near the end of the file.
    private static bool IsValidPdf(ReadOnlySpan<byte> content)
    {
        if (content.Length < 16 || !content[..5].SequenceEqual("%PDF-"u8))
        {
            return false;
        }

        var searchStart = Math.Max(0, content.Length - 2048);
        var eofIndex = content[searchStart..].LastIndexOf("%%EOF"u8);

        if (eofIndex < 0)
        {
            return false;
        }

        var trailing = content[(searchStart + eofIndex + 5)..];

        foreach (var value in trailing)
        {
            if (value is not 0x00 and not 0x09 and not 0x0A and not 0x0C and not 0x0D and not 0x20)
            {
                return false;
            }
        }

        return true;
    }

    // Decodes supported text encodings and rejects invalid byte sequences.
    private static bool TryDecodeText(ReadOnlySpan<byte> content, out string text)
    {
        try
        {
            if (content.StartsWith(new byte[] { 0xEF, 0xBB, 0xBF }))
            {
                text = Utf8WithBom.GetString(content).TrimStart('\uFEFF');
            }
            else if (content.StartsWith(new byte[] { 0xFF, 0xFE }))
            {
                text = new UnicodeEncoding(false, true, true).GetString(content[2..]);
            }
            else if (content.StartsWith(new byte[] { 0xFE, 0xFF }))
            {
                text = new UnicodeEncoding(true, true, true).GetString(content[2..]);
            }
            else
            {
                text = StrictUtf8.GetString(content);
            }

            return true;
        }
        catch (DecoderFallbackException)
        {
            text = string.Empty;
            return false;
        }
    }

    // Rejects empty text and text containing unsupported control characters.
    private static bool IsValidText(ReadOnlySpan<byte> content) =>
        TryDecodeText(content, out var text) &&
        !string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(text) &&
        ContainsOnlyTextCharacters(text);

    // Checks CSV field quoting and line endings after decoding the file.
    private static bool IsValidCsv(ReadOnlySpan<byte> content)
    {
        if (!TryDecodeText(content, out var text) ||
            string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(text) ||
            !ContainsOnlyTextCharacters(text))
        {
            return false;
        }

        var insideQuotes = false;
        var afterQuote = false;
        var atFieldStart = true;

        for (var index = 0; index < text.Length; index++)
        {
            var character = text[index];

            if (insideQuotes)
            {
                if (character == '"')
                {
                    if (index + 1 < text.Length && text[index + 1] == '"')
                    {
                        index++;
                    }
                    else
                    {
                        insideQuotes = false;
                        afterQuote = true;
                    }
                }
                else if (character == '\r' &&
                         (index + 1 >= text.Length || text[index + 1] != '\n'))
                {
                    return false;
                }

                continue;
            }

            if (afterQuote)
            {
                if (character == ',')
                {
                    afterQuote = false;
                    atFieldStart = true;
                    continue;
                }

                if (character == '\n' ||
                    (character == '\r' && index + 1 < text.Length && text[index + 1] == '\n'))
                {
                    if (character == '\r')
                    {
                        index++;
                    }

                    afterQuote = false;
                    atFieldStart = true;
                    continue;
                }

                return false;
            }

            if (character == '"')
            {
                if (!atFieldStart)
                {
                    return false;
                }

                insideQuotes = true;
                atFieldStart = false;
            }
            else if (character == ',')
            {
                atFieldStart = true;
            }
            else if (character == '\n' ||
                     (character == '\r' && index + 1 < text.Length && text[index + 1] == '\n'))
            {
                if (character == '\r')
                {
                    index++;
                }

                atFieldStart = true;
            }
            else
            {
                if (character == '\r')
                {
                    return false;
                }

                atFieldStart = false;
            }
        }

        return !insideQuotes;
    }

    // Allows ordinary text characters, tabs, and line breaks while rejecting other control characters.
    private static bool ContainsOnlyTextCharacters(string text) =>
        text.All(character =>
            !char.IsControl(character) || character is '\t' or '\r' or '\n');

    // Checks Office package parts, expanded size, required XML, content types, and root relationships.
    private static bool IsValidOfficePackage(
        byte[] content,
        string mainPart,
        string mainContentType)
    {
        try
        {
            using var stream = new MemoryStream(content, writable: false);
            using var archive = new ZipArchive(stream, ZipArchiveMode.Read, leaveOpen: true);

            if (archive.Entries.Count > MaximumArchiveEntries)
            {
                return false;
            }

            var parts = new HashSet<string>(StringComparer.Ordinal);
            long expandedLength = 0;

            foreach (var entry in archive.Entries)
            {
                if (!IsSafeArchiveEntryName(entry.FullName) ||
                    entry.Length < 0 ||
                    entry.Length > MaximumExpandedArchiveSize - expandedLength ||
                    !parts.Add(entry.FullName))
                {
                    return false;
                }

                expandedLength += entry.Length;

                using var entryStream = entry.Open();

                if (CopyWithLimit(entryStream, Stream.Null, entry.Length) != entry.Length)
                {
                    return false;
                }
            }

            if (!parts.Contains("[Content_Types].xml") ||
                !parts.Contains("_rels/.rels") ||
                !parts.Contains(mainPart))
            {
                return false;
            }

            var contentTypes = ReadXmlEntry(archive, "[Content_Types].xml");
            var relationships = ReadXmlEntry(archive, "_rels/.rels");
            _ = ReadXmlEntry(archive, mainPart);

            XNamespace contentTypeNamespace =
                "http://schemas.openxmlformats.org/package/2006/content-types";

            XNamespace relationshipNamespace =
                "http://schemas.openxmlformats.org/package/2006/relationships";

            var hasExpectedContentType = contentTypes
                .Root?
                .Elements(contentTypeNamespace + "Override")
                .Any(element =>
                    string.Equals(
                        (string?)element.Attribute("PartName"),
                        $"/{mainPart}",
                        StringComparison.Ordinal) &&
                    string.Equals(
                        (string?)element.Attribute("ContentType"),
                        mainContentType,
                        StringComparison.Ordinal)) == true;

            var hasOfficeDocumentRelationship = relationships
                .Root?
                .Elements(relationshipNamespace + "Relationship")
                .Any(element =>
                    string.Equals(
                        (string?)element.Attribute("Type"),
                        "http://schemas.openxmlformats.org/officeDocument/2006/relationships/officeDocument",
                        StringComparison.Ordinal) &&
                    !string.Equals(
                        (string?)element.Attribute("TargetMode"),
                        "External",
                        StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) &&
                    NormalizePackagePart((string?)element.Attribute("Target")) == mainPart) == true;

            return hasExpectedContentType && hasOfficeDocumentRelationship;
        }
        catch (Exception exception) when (
            exception is IOException or XmlException or ArgumentException or InvalidOperationException)
        {
            return false;
        }
    }

    // Rejects absolute or traversal-like archive paths before processing package entries.
    private static bool IsSafeArchiveEntryName(string name) =>
        !string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(name) &&
        !name.StartsWith("/", StringComparison.Ordinal) &&
        !name.StartsWith('\\') &&
        !name.Contains('\\') &&
        !name.Contains(":", StringComparison.Ordinal) &&
        !name.Split('/').Any(part => part is "." or "..");

    // Reads an Office XML entry without allowing DTD processing or excessive document sizes.
    private static XDocument ReadXmlEntry(ZipArchive archive, string name)
    {
        var entry = archive.GetEntry(name)
            ?? throw new InvalidDataException();

        if (entry.Length > MaximumXmlCharacters)
        {
            throw new InvalidDataException();
        }

        using var stream = entry.Open();

        using var reader = XmlReader.Create(stream, new XmlReaderSettings
        {
            DtdProcessing = DtdProcessing.Prohibit,
            XmlResolver = null,
            MaxCharactersInDocument = MaximumXmlCharacters
        });

        return XDocument.Load(reader);
    }

    // Removes a leading slash from an Office package relationship target.
    private static string NormalizePackagePart(string? target) =>
        (target ?? string.Empty).TrimStart('/');

    // Copies a stream while rejecting content that exceeds its allowed length.
    private static long CopyWithLimit(Stream source, Stream destination, long limit)
    {
        var buffer = new byte[BufferSize];
        long total = 0;
        int count;

        while ((count = source.Read(buffer, 0, buffer.Length)) > 0)
        {
            total += count;

            if (total > limit)
            {
                throw new InvalidDataException();
            }

            destination.Write(buffer, 0, count);
        }

        return total;
    }

    // Checks ZIP structure and limits the number and expanded size of its entries.
    private static bool IsValidZip(ReadOnlySpan<byte> content)
    {
        if (content.Length < 22 ||
            content[0] != (byte)'P' ||
            content[1] != (byte)'K' ||
            content[2] is not 0x03 and not 0x05 and not 0x07)
        {
            return false;
        }

        try
        {
            using var stream = new MemoryStream(content.ToArray(), writable: false);
            using var archive = new ZipArchive(stream, ZipArchiveMode.Read);

            if (archive.Entries.Count > MaximumArchiveEntries)
            {
                return false;
            }

            long expandedLength = 0;

            foreach (var entry in archive.Entries)
            {
                if (!IsSafeArchiveEntryName(entry.FullName) ||
                    entry.Length < 0 ||
                    entry.Length > MaximumExpandedArchiveSize - expandedLength)
                {
                    return false;
                }

                expandedLength += entry.Length;

                using var entryStream = entry.Open();

                if (CopyWithLimit(entryStream, Stream.Null, entry.Length) != entry.Length)
                {
                    return false;
                }
            }

            return true;
        }
        catch (Exception exception) when (
            exception is IOException or ArgumentException or InvalidOperationException)
        {
            return false;
        }
    }

    // Validates ISO Base Media File Format boxes and the expected brands for MP4, MOV, or M4A.
    private static bool IsValidIsoBmff(ReadOnlySpan<byte> content, string format)
    {
        if (content.Length < 24)
        {
            return false;
        }

        var offset = 0;
        var hasFtyp = false;
        var hasMediaData = false;
        var hasMovie = false;
        var compatibleBrands = new HashSet<string>(StringComparer.Ordinal);

        while (offset <= content.Length - 8)
        {
            var boxSize = BinaryPrimitives.ReadUInt32BigEndian(content[offset..]);
            var headerSize = 8;
            ulong actualSize = boxSize;

            if (boxSize == 1)
            {
                if (offset > content.Length - 16)
                {
                    return false;
                }

                actualSize = BinaryPrimitives.ReadUInt64BigEndian(content[(offset + 8)..]);
                headerSize = 16;
            }
            else if (boxSize == 0)
            {
                actualSize = (ulong)(content.Length - offset);
            }

            if (actualSize < (ulong)headerSize ||
                actualSize > (ulong)(content.Length - offset))
            {
                return false;
            }

            var boxType = content.Slice(offset + 4, 4);

            if (boxType.SequenceEqual("ftyp"u8))
            {
                if (hasFtyp ||
                    actualSize < (ulong)(headerSize + 8) ||
                    (actualSize - (ulong)headerSize - 8) % 4 != 0)
                {
                    return false;
                }

                hasFtyp = true;

                var payload = content.Slice(offset + headerSize, (int)actualSize - headerSize);
                compatibleBrands.Add(Encoding.ASCII.GetString(payload[..4]));

                for (var brandOffset = 8; brandOffset + 4 <= payload.Length; brandOffset += 4)
                {
                    compatibleBrands.Add(Encoding.ASCII.GetString(payload.Slice(brandOffset, 4)));
                }
            }
            else if (boxType.SequenceEqual("mdat"u8))
            {
                hasMediaData |= actualSize > (ulong)headerSize;
            }
            else if (boxType.SequenceEqual("moov"u8) || boxType.SequenceEqual("moof"u8))
            {
                hasMovie |= actualSize > (ulong)headerSize;
            }

            offset += (int)actualSize;
        }

        if (offset != content.Length || !hasFtyp || !hasMediaData || !hasMovie)
        {
            return false;
        }

        return format switch
        {
            "mov" => compatibleBrands.Contains("qt  "),
            "m4a" => compatibleBrands.Contains("M4A ") || compatibleBrands.Contains("M4B "),
            _ => compatibleBrands.Overlaps(new[] { "isom", "iso2", "mp41", "mp42", "avc1", "M4V " })
        };
    }

    // Validates the WebM header, document type, segment, and required media elements.
    private static bool IsValidWebM(ReadOnlySpan<byte> content)
    {
        if (content.Length < 32 ||
            !content[..4].SequenceEqual(new byte[] { 0x1A, 0x45, 0xDF, 0xA3 }))
        {
            return false;
        }

        var offset = 4;

        if (!TryReadEbmlVInt(content, ref offset, false, out var headerSize, out var unknownSize) ||
            unknownSize ||
            headerSize > (ulong)(content.Length - offset))
        {
            return false;
        }

        var headerEnd = offset + (int)headerSize;
        var hasWebmDocType = false;

        while (offset < headerEnd)
        {
            if (!TryReadEbmlVInt(content, ref offset, true, out var elementId, out _) ||
                !TryReadEbmlVInt(content, ref offset, false, out var elementSize, out var elementUnknown) ||
                elementUnknown ||
                elementSize > (ulong)(headerEnd - offset))
            {
                return false;
            }

            if (elementId == 0x4282 &&
                Encoding.ASCII.GetString(content.Slice(offset, (int)elementSize)) == "webm")
            {
                hasWebmDocType = true;
            }

            offset += (int)elementSize;
        }

        if (!hasWebmDocType ||
            offset > content.Length - 4 ||
            !TryReadEbmlVInt(content, ref offset, true, out var segmentId, out _) ||
            segmentId != 0x18538067 ||
            !TryReadEbmlVInt(content, ref offset, false, out var segmentSize, out var segmentUnknown))
        {
            return false;
        }

        if (!segmentUnknown && segmentSize > (ulong)(content.Length - offset))
        {
            return false;
        }

        var segmentEnd = segmentUnknown ? content.Length : offset + (int)segmentSize;
        var hasInfo = false;
        var hasTracks = false;
        var hasCluster = false;

        while (offset < segmentEnd)
        {
            if (!TryReadEbmlVInt(content, ref offset, true, out var elementId, out _) ||
                !TryReadEbmlVInt(content, ref offset, false, out var elementSize, out var elementUnknown))
            {
                return false;
            }

            if (elementUnknown)
            {
                if (elementId != 0x1F43B675)
                {
                    return false;
                }

                hasCluster = true;
                offset = segmentEnd;
                break;
            }

            if (elementSize > (ulong)(segmentEnd - offset))
            {
                return false;
            }

            if (elementId == 0x1549A966)
            {
                hasInfo = true;
            }

            if (elementId == 0x1654AE6B)
            {
                hasTracks = true;
            }

            if (elementId == 0x1F43B675)
            {
                hasCluster = true;
            }

            offset += (int)elementSize;
        }

        return offset == content.Length && hasInfo && hasTracks && hasCluster;
    }

    // Reads an EBML variable-length integer and checks its length and unknown-size marker.
    private static bool TryReadEbmlVInt(
        ReadOnlySpan<byte> content,
        ref int offset,
        bool isIdentifier,
        out ulong value,
        out bool unknownSize)
    {
        value = 0;
        unknownSize = false;

        if (offset >= content.Length || content[offset] == 0)
        {
            return false;
        }

        var first = content[offset];
        var length = 1;
        var mask = 0x80;

        while ((first & mask) == 0)
        {
            length++;
            mask >>= 1;
        }

        if (length > (isIdentifier ? 4 : 8) || length > content.Length - offset)
        {
            return false;
        }

        value = isIdentifier ? first : (ulong)(first & (mask - 1));

        for (var index = 1; index < length; index++)
        {
            value = (value << 8) | content[offset + index];
        }

        offset += length;

        if (!isIdentifier)
        {
            var valueBits = length * 7;
            unknownSize = value == (1UL << valueBits) - 1;
        }

        return true;
    }

    // Checks the MP3 frame header and verifies that an audio frame fits within the file.
    private static bool IsValidMp3(ReadOnlySpan<byte> content)
    {
        var offset = 0;

        if (content.Length >= 10 && content[..3].SequenceEqual("ID3"u8))
        {
            if ((content[6] | content[7] | content[8] | content[9]) >= 0x80)
            {
                return false;
            }

            var tagSize = (content[6] << 21) |
                          (content[7] << 14) |
                          (content[8] << 7) |
                          content[9];

            offset = 10 + tagSize + ((content[5] & 0x10) != 0 ? 10 : 0);

            if (offset > content.Length)
            {
                return false;
            }
        }

        while (offset <= content.Length - 4)
        {
            var header = BinaryPrimitives.ReadUInt32BigEndian(content[offset..]);

            if ((header & 0xFFE00000) == 0xFFE00000)
            {
                var version = (int)((header >> 19) & 0x3);
                var layer = (int)((header >> 17) & 0x3);
                var bitrateIndex = (int)((header >> 12) & 0xF);
                var sampleIndex = (int)((header >> 10) & 0x3);

                if (version != 1 && layer == 1 && bitrateIndex is > 0 and < 15 && sampleIndex < 3)
                {
                    var mpeg1Layer3Bitrates = new[]
                    {
                        0, 32, 40, 48, 56, 64, 80, 96, 112, 128, 160, 192, 224, 256, 320
                    };

                    var mpeg2Layer3Bitrates = new[]
                    {
                        0, 8, 16, 24, 32, 40, 48, 56, 64, 80, 96, 112, 128, 144, 160
                    };

                    var sampleRates = version switch
                    {
                        3 => new[] { 44100, 48000, 32000 },
                        2 => new[] { 22050, 24000, 16000 },
                        _ => new[] { 11025, 12000, 8000 }
                    };

                    var bitrate = (version == 3
                        ? mpeg1Layer3Bitrates
                        : mpeg2Layer3Bitrates)[bitrateIndex] * 1000;

                    var frameLength = (version == 3 ? 144 : 72) * bitrate /
                                      sampleRates[sampleIndex] +
                                      (int)((header >> 9) & 1);

                    return frameLength >= 24 && frameLength <= content.Length - offset;
                }
            }

            offset++;
        }

        return false;
    }

    // Checks the WAV header, format chunk, audio data, and declared RIFF size.
    private static bool IsValidWav(ReadOnlySpan<byte> content)
    {
        if (content.Length < 44 ||
            !content[..4].SequenceEqual("RIFF"u8) ||
            !content.Slice(8, 4).SequenceEqual("WAVE"u8) ||
            BinaryPrimitives.ReadUInt32LittleEndian(content[4..]) != content.Length - 8)
        {
            return false;
        }

        var offset = 12;
        var hasFormat = false;
        var hasAudio = false;

        while (offset <= content.Length - 8)
        {
            var chunkSize = BinaryPrimitives.ReadUInt32LittleEndian(content[(offset + 4)..]);

            if (chunkSize > int.MaxValue || chunkSize > content.Length - offset - 8)
            {
                return false;
            }

            var chunkType = content.Slice(offset, 4);
            var chunkData = content.Slice(offset + 8, (int)chunkSize);

            if (chunkType.SequenceEqual("fmt "u8))
            {
                if (chunkSize < 16)
                {
                    return false;
                }

                var channels = BinaryPrimitives.ReadUInt16LittleEndian(chunkData[2..]);
                var sampleRate = BinaryPrimitives.ReadUInt32LittleEndian(chunkData[4..]);
                var blockAlign = BinaryPrimitives.ReadUInt16LittleEndian(chunkData[12..]);
                var bitsPerSample = BinaryPrimitives.ReadUInt16LittleEndian(chunkData[14..]);

                if (channels == 0 || sampleRate == 0 || blockAlign == 0 || bitsPerSample == 0)
                {
                    return false;
                }

                hasFormat = true;
            }
            else if (chunkType.SequenceEqual("data"u8))
            {
                hasAudio |= chunkSize > 0;
            }

            offset += 8 + (int)chunkSize + ((chunkSize & 1) == 1 ? 1 : 0);

            if (offset > content.Length)
            {
                return false;
            }
        }

        return offset == content.Length && hasFormat && hasAudio;
    }
}

import {
  InteractionRequiredAuthError,
  type AccountInfo,
  type IPublicClientApplication,
} from "@azure/msal-browser";

import { apiBaseUrl, apiScope } from "../authConfig";
import { ApiError } from "../types/api";
import type { CurrentUser } from "../types/auth";
import type {
  AlbumResponse,
  CreateAlbumRequest,
  UpdateAlbumRequest,
  AlbumMemberResponse,
  AddAlbumMemberRequest,
  UpdateAlbumMemberRequest,
} from "../types/album";
import type { AdminFileResponse, FileResponse } from "../types/file";

async function acquireAccessToken(
  instance: IPublicClientApplication,
  account: AccountInfo,
): Promise<string> {
  try {
    const result =
      await instance.acquireTokenSilent({
        account,
        scopes: [apiScope],
      });

    if (!result.accessToken) {
      throw new ApiError(
        "Microsoft Entra did not return an access token for the WinCapture API.",
        401,
      );
    }

    return result.accessToken;
  } catch (error) {
    if (error instanceof ApiError) {
      throw error;
    }

    if (
      error instanceof
      InteractionRequiredAuthError
    ) {
      throw new ApiError(
        "Microsoft sign-in is required again before a WinCapture API access token can be acquired.",
        401,
      );
    }

    throw new ApiError(
      "Unable to acquire the Microsoft access token for the WinCapture API.",
      401,
    );
  }
}

async function extractErrorMessage(
  response: Response,
): Promise<string> {
  try {
    const data =
      (await response.json()) as {
        message?: string;
        Message?: string;
        error?: string;
        Error?: string;
        status?: number;
        Status?: number;
      };

    return (
      data.message ||
      data.Message ||
      data.error ||
      data.Error ||
      `Backend returned HTTP ${response.status}.`
    );
  } catch {
    return `Backend returned HTTP ${response.status}.`;
  }
}

export async function callApi(
  instance: IPublicClientApplication,
  account: AccountInfo,
  path: string,
  options: RequestInit = {},
): Promise<Response> {
  const accessToken =
    await acquireAccessToken(
      instance,
      account,
    );

  const headers =
    new Headers(options.headers);

  headers.set(
    "Authorization",
    `Bearer ${accessToken}`,
  );

  if (
    options.body &&
    !(options.body instanceof FormData)
  ) {
    headers.set(
      "Content-Type",
      "application/json",
    );
  }

  let response: Response;

  try {
    response = await fetch(
      `${apiBaseUrl}${path}`,
      {
        ...options,
        headers,
      },
    );
  } catch {
    throw new ApiError(
      "The WinCapture API could not be reached. Ensure the backend is running and reachable.",
      0,
    );
  }

  if (!response.ok) {
    const serverMessage =
      await extractErrorMessage(
        response,
      );

    let message =
      serverMessage;

    if (response.status === 400) {
      message =
        `Validation failed: ${serverMessage}`;
    } else if (
      response.status === 401
    ) {
      message =
        `Authentication expired or invalid: ${serverMessage}`;
    } else if (
      response.status === 403
    ) {
      message =
        `Access denied: ${serverMessage}`;
    } else if (
      response.status === 404
    ) {
      message =
        `Resource not found: ${serverMessage}`;
    } else if (
      response.status === 409
    ) {
      message =
        `Conflict: ${serverMessage}`;
    } else if (
      response.status === 413
    ) {
      message =
        `File too large: ${serverMessage}`;
    } else if (
      response.status >= 500
    ) {
      message =
        "An unexpected server error occurred. Please try again later.";
    }

    throw new ApiError(
      message,
      response.status,
    );
  }

  return response;
}

  instance: IPublicClientApplication,
  account: AccountInfo,
): Promise<CurrentUser> {
  const response =
    await callApi(
      instance,
      account,
      "/api/auth/me",
    );

  return (
    await response.json()
  ) as CurrentUser;
}

export async function getFilePreviewUrl(
  instance: IPublicClientApplication,
  account: AccountInfo,
  fileId: number,
  albumId?: number,
  canManage = false,
): Promise<string> {
  const path =
    albumId
      ? canManage
        ? `/api/albums/${albumId}/files/${fileId}/download`
        : `/api/albums/${albumId}/files/${fileId}/preview`
      : `/api/files/${fileId}/download`;

  return getFilePreviewUrlByPath(
    instance,
    account,
    path,
  );
}

export async function getFilePreviewUrlByPath(
  instance: IPublicClientApplication,
  account: AccountInfo,
  path: string,
  contentType?: string,
): Promise<string> {
  const response =
    await callApi(
      instance,
      account,
      path,
    );

  const responseBlob =
    await response.blob();

  const previewBlob =
    contentType
      ? new Blob(
          [responseBlob],
          {
            type: contentType,
          },
        )
      : responseBlob;

  return URL.createObjectURL(
    previewBlob,
  );
}

export async function deleteAlbumFile(
  instance: IPublicClientApplication,

  await callApi(
    instance,
    account,
    `/api/albums/${albumId}/files/${fileId}`,
    {
      method: "DELETE",
    },
  );
}

async function uploadMultipartFile<T>(
  instance: IPublicClientApplication,
  account: AccountInfo,
  path: string,
  method: "POST" | "PUT",
  file: File,
): Promise<T> {
  const formData = new FormData();
  formData.append("file", file);

  const response = await callApi(
    instance,
    account,
    path,
    {
      method,
      body: formData,
    },
  );

  return (await response.json()) as T;
}

async function downloadResponse(
  instance: IPublicClientApplication,
  account: AccountInfo,
  path: string,
  fallbackFileName: string,
): Promise<void> {
  const response = await callApi(
    instance,
    account,
    path,
  );

  const blob = await response.blob();
  const fileName = extractFileNameFromHeaders(
    response,
    fallbackFileName,
  );

  triggerBrowserDownload(blob, fileName);
}

// ==========================================
// Binary Download Utilities
// ==========================================

function extractFileNameFromHeaders(
  response: Response,
  fallback: string,
): string {
  const disposition =
    response.headers.get(
      "content-disposition",
    );

  if (!disposition) {
    return fallback;
  }

  const match =
    disposition.match(
      /filename\*?=(?:UTF-8'')?["']?([^"';]+)["']?/i,
    );

  return match?.[1]
    ? decodeURIComponent(
        match[1],
      )
    : fallback;
}

function triggerBrowserDownload(
  blob: Blob,
  filename: string,
): void {
  const url =
    window.URL.createObjectURL(
      blob,
    );

  const anchor =
    document.createElement("a");

  anchor.href = url;
  anchor.download = filename;

  document.body.appendChild(
    anchor,
  );

  anchor.click();

  document.body.removeChild(
    anchor,
  );

  window.URL.revokeObjectURL(
    url,
  );
}

import {
  useEffect,
  useRef,
} from "react";
import {
  Navigate,
  Route,
  Routes,
} from "react-router-dom";

import { useAuth } from "./context/AuthContext";
import {
  brandMarkClass,
  brandMarkLetterClass,
} from "./components/ui/uiClasses";
import Layout from "./components/Layout";

import AlbumDetailPage from "./pages/AlbumDetailPage";
import FilesPage from "./pages/FilesPage";
import AdminPage from "./pages/AdminPage";

function AuthGate() {
  const {
    account,
    user,
    loading,
    login,
    logout,
    error,
    refreshUser,
  } = useAuth();
  const redirectStarted = useRef(false);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!loading && !error && !account && !user && !redirectStarted.current) {
      redirectStarted.current = true;
      void login();
    }
    if (account || user) redirectStarted.current = false;
  }, [account, error, loading, login, user]);

  if (loading) {
    return (
      <main className="relative isolate grid min-h-screen place-items-center overflow-hidden bg-[radial-gradient(circle_at_20%_18%,rgba(255,255,255,0.98),transparent_28%),radial-gradient(circle_at_82%_75%,rgba(117,190,255,0.22),transparent_31%),linear-gradient(145deg,#f6faff_0%,#e5f1fc_49%,#f6f7fb_100%)] p-6 max-[520px]:p-[17px]">
        <div className="pointer-events-none absolute right-[-90px] top-[-170px] z-[-1] h-[340px] w-[340px] rounded-full bg-[rgba(86,169,242,0.16)] blur-[1px]" />
        <div className="pointer-events-none absolute bottom-[-150px] left-[-100px] z-[-1] h-[280px] w-[280px] rounded-full bg-[rgba(103,181,247,0.13)] blur-[1px]" />

        <section className="surface relative w-[min(460px,100%)] rounded-[23px] p-[30px] shadow-[0_24px_60px_rgba(44,93,135,0.11),inset_0_1px_rgba(255,255,255,0.86)] max-[520px]:rounded-[19px] max-[520px]:px-5 max-[520px]:py-[23px]">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-[11px]">
            <span className={brandMarkClass} aria-hidden="true">
              <span className={brandMarkLetterClass}>W</span>
            </span>

            <div className="grid gap-0.5">
              <strong className="text-[15px] font-bold tracking-[-0.02em] text-[#173653]">
                WinCapture
              </strong>

              <small className="text-[9px] font-medium text-[#8498aa]">
                WinWire
              </small>
            </div>
          </div>

          <div className="mt-[42px] flex items-center gap-[14px] max-[520px]:mt-[34px]">
            <span className="h-[42px] w-[42px] flex-none animate-[auth-spin_850ms_linear_infinite] rounded-full border-[3px] border-[#dce8f1] border-t-[#2c8bea] motion-reduce:animate-none" />

                Connecting to Microsoft

                WinCapture workspace.

            <span className="block h-full w-[42%] animate-[auth-progress_1.6s_ease-in-out_infinite] rounded-[inherit] bg-[linear-gradient(90deg,#61b4ff,#1777ed)] motion-reduce:animate-none" />
          </div>

          <span className="mt-[14px] block text-center text-[8px] font-medium text-[#98a8b5]">
            Microsoft Entra ID authentication
          </span>
        </section>
      </main>
    );
  }

  if (error) {
    return (
      <main className="grid min-h-screen place-items-center bg-[#f3f8fc] p-6">
        <section className="surface w-[min(460px,100%)] rounded-[20px] p-7">
          <h1 className="text-lg font-semibold text-[#183a57]">Unable to connect to WinCapture</h1>
          <p role="alert" className="mt-3 text-sm text-[#60798e]">{error}</p>
          <div className="mt-5 flex gap-3">
            <button className="primary-action" type="button" onClick={() => void (account ? refreshUser() : login())}>Retry</button>
            <button className="secondary-action" type="button" onClick={() => void logout()}>Sign out</button>
          </div>
        </section>
      </main>
    );
  }

  if (!account && !user) {
    return (
      <main className="relative isolate grid min-h-screen place-items-center overflow-hidden bg-[radial-gradient(circle_at_20%_18%,rgba(255,255,255,0.98),transparent_28%),radial-gradient(circle_at_82%_75%,rgba(117,190,255,0.22),transparent_31%),linear-gradient(145deg,#f6faff_0%,#e5f1fc_49%,#f6f7fb_100%)] p-6 max-[520px]:p-[17px]">
        <div className="pointer-events-none absolute right-[-90px] top-[-170px] z-[-1] h-[340px] w-[340px] rounded-full bg-[rgba(86,169,242,0.16)] blur-[1px]" />

          <span className="mt-[14px] block text-center text-[8px] font-medium text-[#98a8b5]">

  return null;
}

export default function App() {
  const {
    user,
    loading,
  } = useAuth();

  if (loading && !user) {
    return <AuthGate />;
  }

  if (!user) {
    return <AuthGate />;
  }

  return (
    <Routes>
      <Route element={<Layout />}>
        <Route
          path="/"
          element={
            <Navigate
              to="/dashboard"
              replace
            />
          }
        />

        <Route
          path="/dashboard"
          element={<Dashboard />}
        />

        <Route
          path="/albums"
          element={<AlbumsPage />}
        />

        <Route
          path="/albums/:albumId"
          element={
            <AlbumDetailPage />
          }
        />

        <Route
          path="/files"
          element={<FilesPage />}
        />

        {user.role === "Admin" && (
          <Route
            path="/admin"
            element={<AdminPage />}
          />
        )}
      </Route>

      <Route
        path="*"
        element={
          <Navigate
            to="/dashboard"
            replace
          />
        }
      />
    </Routes>
  );
}

import type { Configuration, RedirectRequest } from "@azure/msal-browser";

export const apiBaseUrl = import.meta.env.VITE_API_BASE_URL?.trim() ?? "";
export const msalClientId = import.meta.env.VITE_MSAL_CLIENT_ID?.trim() ?? "";
export const msalTenantId = import.meta.env.VITE_MSAL_TENANT_ID?.trim() ?? "";
export const msalRedirectUri =
  import.meta.env.VITE_MSAL_REDIRECT_URI?.trim() || "https://localhost:5173/";
export const msalPostLogoutRedirectUri =
  import.meta.env.VITE_MSAL_POST_LOGOUT_REDIRECT_URI?.trim() || "https://localhost:5173/";
export const apiScope = import.meta.env.VITE_MSAL_API_SCOPE?.trim() ?? "";

export function validateEnvironment(): string[] {
  const missing: string[] = [];

  if (!apiBaseUrl) {
    missing.push("VITE_API_BASE_URL");
  }

  if (!msalClientId) {
    missing.push("VITE_MSAL_CLIENT_ID");
  }

  if (!msalTenantId) {
    missing.push("VITE_MSAL_TENANT_ID");
  }

  if (!msalRedirectUri) {
    missing.push("VITE_MSAL_REDIRECT_URI");
  }

  if (!apiScope) {
    missing.push("VITE_MSAL_API_SCOPE");
  }

  return missing;
}

export const msalConfig: Configuration = {
  auth: {
    clientId: msalClientId,
    authority: `https://login.microsoftonline.com/${msalTenantId}`,
    redirectUri: msalRedirectUri,
    postLogoutRedirectUri: msalPostLogoutRedirectUri,
  },
  cache: {
    cacheLocation: "sessionStorage",
  },
};

export const loginRequest: RedirectRequest = {
  scopes: [apiScope],
  redirectStartPage: "/",
};

import {
  FileText,
  Star,
} from "lucide-react";

import FileActionMenu from "./files/FileActionMenu";
import type { FileResponse } from "../types/file";
import {
  formatDate,
  formatFileSize,
  formatFileType,
} from "../utils/formatters";

interface FileCardProps {
  file: FileResponse;
  previewUrl?: string;
  canManage?: boolean;
  favoritePending?: boolean;
  onFavoriteToggle: () => void;
  onReplace: () => void;
  onDetails: () => void;
  onPreview: () => void;
  onDownload: () => void;
  onDelete: () => void;
}

function getFileCategory(contentType: string): string {
  if (contentType.startsWith("image/")) {
    return "image";
  }

  if (contentType === "application/pdf") {
    return "document";
  }

  return "file";
}

export default function FileCard({
  file,
  previewUrl,
  canManage,
  favoritePending = false,
  onFavoriteToggle,
  onReplace,
  onDetails,
  onPreview,
  onDownload,
  onDelete,
}: FileCardProps) {
  const isImage =
    file.contentType.startsWith("image/");

  const effectiveCanManage =
    canManage ?? file.canManage;

  const category =
    getFileCategory(file.contentType);

  return (
    <article className="surface group min-w-0 overflow-visible rounded-[17px] transition-[transform,border-color,box-shadow] duration-200 hover:-translate-y-[3px] hover:border-[rgba(178,207,231,0.68)] hover:shadow-[0_17px_34px_rgba(49,103,149,0.12),inset_0_0_0_1px_rgba(201,222,237,0.22)]">
      <div
        className="relative m-[10px] h-[176px] min-w-0 overflow-hidden rounded-[13px] bg-[linear-gradient(145deg,#eef7fe,#e4f0f8)] max-[520px]:h-[190px]"
      >
        {isImage && previewUrl ? (
          <img
            src={previewUrl}
            alt={file.originalFileName}
            loading="lazy"
            role="button"
            tabIndex={0}
            aria-label={`Open full-screen preview of ${file.originalFileName}`}
            onClick={onPreview}
            onKeyDown={(event) => { if (event.key === "Enter" || event.key === " ") { event.preventDefault(); onPreview(); } }}
            className="h-full w-full cursor-zoom-in object-cover transition-[transform,filter] duration-[350ms] group-hover:scale-[1.04] group-hover:saturate-[1.03]"
          />
        ) : (
          <div className={`flex h-full w-full flex-col items-center justify-center gap-2 ${category === "document" ? "bg-[linear-gradient(145deg,#f1efff,#e7e8ff)] text-[#5d67c9]" : category === "file" ? "bg-[linear-gradient(145deg,#eef6fa,#e7f0f4)]" : ""}`}>
            <span className={`grid h-[57px] w-[57px] place-items-center rounded-[17px] bg-[rgba(255,255,255,0.66)] text-[#3788da] shadow-[0_9px_20px_rgba(49,109,161,0.07),inset_0_1px_rgba(255,255,255,0.76)] ${category === "document" ? "text-[#656fd3]" : ""}`}>
              <FileText
                aria-hidden="true"
                size={38}
                strokeWidth={1.45}
              />
            </span>

            <span className="rounded-full bg-[rgba(255,255,255,0.56)] px-[7px] py-1 text-[8px] font-bold tracking-[0.08em] text-[#608099]">
              {formatFileType(file.contentType)}
            </span>
          </div>
        )}

        <div className="pointer-events-none absolute inset-0 bg-[linear-gradient(180deg,rgba(11,42,68,0.08),transparent_30%,rgba(11,42,68,0.16))]" />

        <button
          type="button"
          aria-label={
            file.isFavorite
              ? `Remove ${file.originalFileName} from favorites`
              : `Add ${file.originalFileName} to favorites`
          }
          aria-pressed={file.isFavorite}
          title={
            file.isFavorite
              ? "Remove from favorites"
              : "Add to favorites"
          }
          disabled={favoritePending}
          onClick={onFavoriteToggle}
          className={`absolute right-[9px] top-[9px] z-[2] grid h-[34px] w-[34px] cursor-pointer place-items-center rounded-full border border-[rgba(255,255,255,0.75)] bg-[rgba(255,255,255,0.76)] text-[#627d92] shadow-[0_7px_16px_rgba(27,76,119,0.1),inset_0_1px_rgba(255,255,255,0.85)] backdrop-blur-[9px] transition-[transform,color,background] duration-[180ms] hover:enabled:scale-[1.06] hover:enabled:bg-[rgba(255,255,255,0.9)] hover:enabled:text-[#da8b16] disabled:opacity-[0.55] ${
            file.isFavorite
              ? "text-[#e79a1b]"
              : ""
          }`}
        >
          <Star
            aria-hidden="true"
            size={17}
            strokeWidth={1.8}
            className={
              file.isFavorite
                ? "fill-current text-[#e79a1b]"
                : ""
            }
          />
        </button>

        <div className="absolute bottom-[9px] left-[9px] z-[2] rounded-full border border-[rgba(255,255,255,0.22)] bg-[rgba(10,36,57,0.33)] px-[7px] py-1 text-[8px] font-bold tracking-[0.06em] text-[rgba(255,255,255,0.96)] backdrop-blur-[7px]">
          {formatFileType(file.contentType)}
        </div>
      </div>

      <div className="min-w-0 px-[13px] pt-px pb-3">
        <div className="min-w-0">
          <div className="flex items-start justify-between gap-2">
            <div className="min-w-0">
              <h3
                className="m-0 overflow-hidden text-ellipsis whitespace-nowrap text-[12px] leading-[1.3] font-[650] text-[#193c59]"
                title={file.originalFileName}
              >
                {file.originalFileName}
              </h3>

              <p className="mt-[5px] flex items-center gap-[5px] text-[8px] text-[#8295a5]">
                {formatFileType(file.contentType)}

                <span aria-hidden="true">
                  {"\u00B7"}
                </span>

                {formatFileSize(file.fileSize)}
              </p>
            </div>

            <FileActionMenu
              fileName={file.originalFileName}
              canManage={effectiveCanManage}
              onDetails={onDetails}
              onReplace={onReplace}
              onDownload={onDownload}
              onDelete={onDelete}
            />
          </div>

          <p className="mt-[10px] mb-0 border-t border-[#ebf1f5] pt-[9px] text-[8px] text-[#9aaab7]">
            Added {formatDate(file.uploadedAt)}
          </p>
        </div>

      </div>
    </article>
  );
}

FILE: C:\Users\ShodhanVemulapalli\Downloads\WinCapture\frontend\src\components\files\FileActionMenu.tsx
============================================================

import {
  useCallback,
  useEffect,
  useLayoutEffect,
  useRef,
  useState,
} from "react";
import {
  Download,
  Info,
  MoreHorizontal,
  Replace,
  Trash2,
} from "lucide-react";
import { createPortal } from "react-dom";

interface FileActionMenuProps {
  fileName: string;
  canManage: boolean;
  onReplace: () => void;
  onDetails: () => void;
  onDownload: () => void;
  onDelete: () => void;
}

interface MenuPosition {
  top: number;
  left: number;
}

const MENU_GAP = 6;
const VIEWPORT_MARGIN = 8;

export default function FileActionMenu({
  fileName,
  canManage,
  onReplace,
  onDetails,
  onDownload,
  onDelete,
}: FileActionMenuProps) {
  const [open, setOpen] = useState(false);

  const [position, setPosition] =
    useState<MenuPosition>({
      top: 0,
      left: 0,
    });

  const triggerRef =
    useRef<HTMLButtonElement | null>(null);

  const menuRef =
    useRef<HTMLDivElement | null>(null);

  const positionMenu = useCallback(() => {
    if (
      !triggerRef.current ||
      !menuRef.current
    ) {
      return;
    }

    const triggerRect =
      triggerRef.current.getBoundingClientRect();

    const menuRect =
      menuRef.current.getBoundingClientRect();

    const maxLeft = Math.max(
      VIEWPORT_MARGIN,
      window.innerWidth - menuRect.width - VIEWPORT_MARGIN,
    );
    const maxTop = Math.max(
      VIEWPORT_MARGIN,
      window.innerHeight - menuRect.height - VIEWPORT_MARGIN,
    );
    const belowTop = triggerRect.bottom + MENU_GAP;
    const aboveTop = triggerRect.top - menuRect.height - MENU_GAP;
    const fitsBelow = belowTop <= maxTop;
    const top = fitsBelow
      ? belowTop
      : Math.max(VIEWPORT_MARGIN, Math.min(aboveTop, maxTop));
    const left = Math.max(
      VIEWPORT_MARGIN,
      Math.min(triggerRect.right - menuRect.width, maxLeft),
    );

    setPosition({
      top,
      left,
    });
  }, []);

  useLayoutEffect(() => {
    if (!open) {
      return;
    }

    menuRef.current
      ?.querySelector<HTMLButtonElement>('[role="menuitem"]')
      ?.focus();

    positionMenu();
  }, [open, positionMenu]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!open) {
      return;
    }

    const handleReposition = () => {
      positionMenu();
    };

    const handlePointerDown = (
      event: PointerEvent,
    ) => {
      const target = event.target;

      if (
        target instanceof Node &&
        !triggerRef.current?.contains(
          target,
        ) &&
        !menuRef.current?.contains(
          target,
        )
      ) {
        setOpen(false);
      }
    };

    const handleKeyDown = (
      event: KeyboardEvent,
    ) => {
      if (event.key === "Escape") {
        setOpen(false);
        triggerRef.current?.focus();
      }
    };

    window.addEventListener(
      "resize",
      handleReposition,
    );

    window.addEventListener(
      "scroll",
      handleReposition,
      true,
    );

    document.addEventListener(
      "pointerdown",
      handlePointerDown,
    );

    document.addEventListener(
      "keydown",
      handleKeyDown,
    );

    return () => {
      window.removeEventListener(
        "resize",
        handleReposition,
      );

      window.removeEventListener(
        "scroll",
        handleReposition,
        true,
      );

      document.removeEventListener(
        "pointerdown",
        handlePointerDown,
      );

      document.removeEventListener(
        "keydown",
        handleKeyDown,
      );
    };
  }, [open, positionMenu]);

  const runAction = (
    action: () => void,
  ) => {
    setOpen(false);
    action();
  };

  return (
    <>
      <button
        ref={triggerRef}
        type="button"
        className="grid h-[30px] w-[30px] flex-none place-items-center rounded-[9px] border-0 bg-transparent p-0 font-[inherit] text-[#8297a8] transition-[color,background] duration-[180ms] ease-[ease] hover:bg-[#edf6fd] hover:text-[#2b73bf] aria-expanded:bg-[#eaf4fc] aria-expanded:text-[#2876c7]"
        onClick={() =>
          setOpen(
            (current) => !current,
          )
        }
        aria-label={`Actions for ${fileName}`}
        aria-haspopup="menu"
        aria-expanded={open}
        title="File actions"
      >
        <MoreHorizontal
          aria-hidden="true"
          size={19}
          strokeWidth={1.8}
        />
      </button>

      {open &&
        createPortal(
          <div
            ref={menuRef}
            className="z-[1000] w-[148px] max-h-[calc(100dvh-16px)] overflow-y-auto rounded-[11px] border border-[rgba(199,222,239,0.86)] bg-[rgba(255,255,255,0.92)] p-[5px] shadow-[0_16px_34px_rgba(37,78,111,0.13),inset_0_1px_rgba(255,255,255,0.88)] backdrop-blur-[16px]"
            role="menu"
            style={{
              position: "fixed",
              top: position.top,
              left: position.left,
            }}
          >
            <button
              type="button"
              className="flex min-h-[34px] w-full items-center gap-2 rounded-lg border-0 bg-transparent px-[9px] text-left text-[10px] font-medium text-[#5d7489] transition-[color,background] duration-[160ms] ease-[ease] hover:bg-[#edf6fd] hover:text-[#1c68b5]"
              role="menuitem"
              onClick={() => runAction(onDetails)}
            >
              <Info aria-hidden="true" size={16} strokeWidth={1.8} />
              <span>Details</span>
            </button>

            {canManage && (
              <button
                type="button"
                className="flex min-h-[34px] w-full items-center gap-2 rounded-lg border-0 bg-transparent px-[9px] text-left text-[10px] font-medium text-[#5d7489] transition-[color,background] duration-[160ms] ease-[ease] hover:bg-[#edf6fd] hover:text-[#1c68b5]"
                role="menuitem"
                onClick={() =>
                  runAction(onReplace)
                }
              >
                <Replace
                  aria-hidden="true"
                  size={16}
                  strokeWidth={1.8}
                />

                <span>Replace</span>
              </button>
            )}

            <button
              type="button"
              className="flex min-h-[34px] w-full items-center gap-2 rounded-lg border-0 bg-transparent px-[9px] text-left text-[10px] font-medium text-[#5d7489] transition-[color,background] duration-[160ms] ease-[ease] hover:bg-[#edf6fd] hover:text-[#1c68b5]"
              role="menuitem"
              onClick={() =>
                runAction(onDownload)
              }
            >
              <Download
                aria-hidden="true"
                size={16}
                strokeWidth={1.8}
              />

              <span>Download</span>
            </button>

            {canManage && (
              <button
                type="button"
                className="flex min-h-[34px] w-full items-center gap-2 rounded-lg border-0 bg-transparent px-[9px] text-left text-[10px] font-medium text-[#d85059] transition-[color,background] duration-[160ms] ease-[ease] hover:bg-[#fff0f1] hover:text-[#c33d46]"
                role="menuitem"
                onClick={() =>
                  runAction(onDelete)
                }
              >
                <Trash2
                  aria-hidden="true"
                  size={16}
                  strokeWidth={1.8}
                />

                <span>Delete</span>
              </button>
            )}
          </div>,
          document.body,
        )}
    </>
  );
}

import type { ReactNode } from "react";

import FileCard from "../FileCard";
import type { FileResponse } from "../../types/file";

interface FileCardGridProps {
  files: FileResponse[];
  previewUrls: Record<number, string>;
  favoritePendingIds: ReadonlySet<number>;
  canManage?: boolean | ((file: FileResponse) => boolean);
  onFavoriteToggle: (file: FileResponse) => void;
  onReplace: (file: FileResponse) => void;
  onDetails: (file: FileResponse) => void;
  onPreview: (file: FileResponse) => void;
  onDownload: (file: FileResponse) => void;
  onDelete: (file: FileResponse) => void;
  empty?: ReactNode;
}

export default function FileCardGrid({
  files,
  previewUrls,
  favoritePendingIds,
  canManage = false,
  onFavoriteToggle,
  onReplace,
  onDetails,
  onPreview,
  onDownload,
  onDelete,
  empty,
}: FileCardGridProps) {
  if (files.length === 0) {
    return <>{empty}</>;
  }

  return (
    <div className="grid grid-cols-3 gap-[17px] max-[1200px]:grid-cols-2 max-[760px]:grid-cols-2 max-[760px]:gap-3 max-[520px]:grid-cols-1">
      {files.map((file) => {
        const effectiveCanManage =
          typeof canManage === "function"
            ? canManage(file)
            : canManage;

        return (
          <FileCard
            key={file.id}
            file={file}
            previewUrl={previewUrls[file.id]}
            canManage={effectiveCanManage}
            favoritePending={favoritePendingIds.has(
              file.id,
            )}
            onFavoriteToggle={() =>
              onFavoriteToggle(file)
            }
            onReplace={() => onReplace(file)}
            onDetails={() => onDetails(file)}
            onPreview={() => onPreview(file)}
            onDownload={() => onDownload(file)}
            onDelete={() => onDelete(file)}
          />
        );
      })}
    </div>
  );
}
