import { useEffect } from "react";
import { X } from "lucide-react";
import type { FileResponse } from "../../types/file";

export default function ImagePreviewModal({ file, src, onClose }: { file: FileResponse; src?: string; onClose: () => void }) {
  useEffect(() => {
    const closeOnEscape = (event: KeyboardEvent) => { if (event.key === "Escape") onClose(); };
    window.addEventListener("keydown", closeOnEscape);
    return () => window.removeEventListener("keydown", closeOnEscape);
  }, [onClose]);
  return <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-center justify-center bg-black/90 p-5" role="dialog" aria-modal="true" aria-label={`Preview ${file.originalFileName}`} onMouseDown={(event) => { if (event.target === event.currentTarget) onClose(); }}>
    <button type="button" onClick={onClose} aria-label="Close image preview" className="absolute right-5 top-5 rounded-full bg-white/15 p-3 text-white hover:bg-white/25"><X aria-hidden="true" /></button>
    {src ? <img src={src} alt={file.originalFileName} className="max-h-[90vh] max-w-[95vw] object-contain" /> : <p className="text-white">Preview unavailable</p>}
  </div>;
}

const navLinkClass = (active: boolean) =>
  `group relative flex min-h-[44px] w-full cursor-pointer items-center gap-3 rounded-xl border border-transparent bg-transparent px-3 text-[13px] font-medium text-[#57728a] transition-[color,background,border-color,transform,box-shadow] duration-[180ms] hover:border-[rgba(180,209,235,0.34)] hover:bg-[rgba(226,239,251,0.72)] hover:text-[#126dd7] active:translate-y-px ${active ? interface PaginationProps {
  currentPage: number;
  pageCount: number;
  onPageChange: (page: number) => void;
}

export default function Pagination({
  currentPage,
  pageCount,
  onPageChange,
}: PaginationProps) {
  return (
    <nav
      aria-label="Pagination"
      className="flex items-center justify-between gap-3 border-t border-[#e6eef4] px-[15px] py-3 text-[9px] text-[#70879a] max-[520px]:px-3"
    >
      <span>Page {currentPage} of {pageCount}</span>
      <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
        <button
          type="button"
          onClick={() => onPageChange(currentPage - 1)}
          disabled={currentPage <= 1}
          className="rounded-[8px] border border-[#dce8f0] bg-[rgba(255,255,255,0.62)] px-[10px] py-[6px] font-semibold text-[#4e6d85] transition-colors hover:bg-[#edf6fd] disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:opacity-45"
        >
          Previous
        </button>
        <button
          type="button"
          onClick={() => onPageChange(currentPage + 1)}
          disabled={currentPage >= pageCount}
          className="rounded-[8px] border border-[#dce8f0] bg-[rgba(255,255,255,0.62)] px-[10px] py-[6px] font-semibold text-[#4e6d85] transition-colors hover:bg-[#edf6fd] disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:opacity-45"
        >
          Next
        </button>
      </div>
    </nav>
  );
}

FILE: C:\Users\ShodhanVemulapalli\Downloads\WinCapture\frontend\src\context\AuthContext.tsx
============================================================

import {
  createContext,
  useContext,
  useCallback,
  useEffect,
  useMemo,
  useRef,
  useState,
  type ReactNode,
} from "react";

import type {
  AccountInfo,
} from "@azure/msal-browser";

import {
  InteractionStatus,
} from "@azure/msal-browser";

import {
  useMsal,
} from "@azure/msal-react";

import {
  getCurrentUser,
} from "../api/apiClient";
import type { CurrentUser } from "../types/auth";

import {
  loginRequest,
  msalPostLogoutRedirectUri,
} from "../authConfig";

interface AuthContextValue {
  account: AccountInfo | null;
  user: CurrentUser | null;
  loading: boolean;
  error: string | null;
  login: () => Promise<void>;
  logout: () => Promise<void>;
  refreshUser: () => Promise<void>;
}

const AuthContext =
  createContext<AuthContextValue | undefined>(
    undefined,
  );

export function AuthProvider({
  children,
}: {
  children: ReactNode;
}) {
  const {
    instance,
    accounts,
    inProgress,
  } = useMsal();

  const [user, setUser] =
    useState<CurrentUser | null>(null);

  const [loading, setLoading] =
    useState(true);

  const [error, setError] =
    useState<string | null>(null);
  const requestVersion = useRef(0);
  const profileAccountId = useRef<string | null>(null);
  const refreshUserRef = useRef<() => Promise<void>>(() => Promise.resolve());

  useEffect(() => {
    if (
      !instance.getActiveAccount() &&
      accounts.length > 0
    ) {
      instance.setActiveAccount(
        accounts[0],
      );
    }
  }, [instance, accounts]);

  const account =
    instance.getActiveAccount() ??
    accounts[0] ??
    null;

  const refreshUser = useCallback(async () => {
      const version = ++requestVersion.current;
      if (!account) {
        profileAccountId.current = null;
        setUser(null);
        setLoading(false);
        setError(null);
        return;
      }

      if (profileAccountId.current !== account.homeAccountId) {
        profileAccountId.current = null;
        setUser(null);
      }
      setLoading(true);
      setError(null);

      try {
        const currentUser =
          await getCurrentUser(
            instance,
            account,
          );

        if (requestVersion.current === version) {
          profileAccountId.current = account.homeAccountId;
          setUser(currentUser);
        }
      } catch (caughtError) {
        if (requestVersion.current === version) {
          profileAccountId.current = null;
          setUser(null);
          setError(
          caughtError instanceof Error
            ? caughtError.message
            : "Microsoft authentication succeeded, but the WinCapture API rejected the request.",
          );
        }
      } finally {
        if (requestVersion.current === version) setLoading(false);
      }
    }, [account, instance]);

  refreshUserRef.current = refreshUser;

  useEffect(() => {
    if (
      inProgress ===
      InteractionStatus.None
    ) {
      void refreshUserRef.current();
    }
  }, [
    account?.homeAccountId,
    inProgress,
  ]);

  const login = useCallback(async () => {
      setError(null);

      try {
        await instance.loginRedirect(
          loginRequest,
        );
      } catch (caughtError) {
        setError(
          caughtError instanceof Error
            ? caughtError.message
            : "Microsoft authentication could not be started.",
        );
      }
    }, [instance]);

  const logout = useCallback(async () => {
      requestVersion.current += 1;
      profileAccountId.current = null;
      setUser(null);
      setError(null);

      try {
        await instance.logoutRedirect({
          account:
            account ?? undefined,
          postLogoutRedirectUri:
            msalPostLogoutRedirectUri,
        });
      } catch (caughtError) {
        setError(
          caughtError instanceof Error
            ? caughtError.message
            : "Microsoft logout could not be completed.",
        );
      }
    }, [account, instance]);

  const value =
    useMemo<AuthContextValue>(
      () => ({
        account,
        user,
        loading:
          loading ||
          inProgress !==
            InteractionStatus.None,
        error,
        login,
        logout,
        refreshUser,
      }),
      [
        account,
        user,
        loading,
        inProgress,
        error,
        login,
        logout,
        refreshUser,
      ],
    );

  return (
    <AuthContext.Provider
      value={value}
    >
      {children}
    </AuthContext.Provider>
  );
}

export function useAuth(): AuthContextValue {
  const context =
    useContext(AuthContext);

  if (!context) {
    throw new Error(
      "useAuth must be used inside AuthProvider.",
    );
  }

  return context;
}

FILE: C:\Users\ShodhanVemulapalli\Downloads\WinCapture\frontend\src\hooks\useAlbumCoverPreviews.ts
============================================================

import { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import type { AccountInfo, IPublicClientApplication } from "@azure/msal-browser";

import { getAlbumFiles, getFilePreviewUrl } from "../api/apiClient";
import type { AlbumResponse } from "../types/album";

export default function useAlbumCoverPreviews(
  albums: AlbumResponse[],
  instance: IPublicClientApplication,
  account: AccountInfo | null,
  userId?: number,
  isAdmin = false,
): Record<number, string> {
  const [coverUrls, setCoverUrls] = useState<Record<number, string>>({});

  useEffect(() => {
    let active = true;
    let cleanupRequested = false;
    const createdUrls: string[] = [];

    const registerUrl = (url: string) => {
      if (cleanupRequested) {
        URL.revokeObjectURL(url);
      } else {
        createdUrls.push(url);
      }
    };

    setCoverUrls({});

    if (!account || albums.length === 0) {
      return () => {
        cleanupRequested = true;
      };
    }

    const load = async () => {
      const entries = await Promise.all(
        albums.map(async (album) => {
          try {
            const files = await getAlbumFiles(instance, account, album.id);
            const image = files.find((file) => file.contentType.startsWith("image/"));
            if (!image) return null;

            const canManage = isAdmin || album.ownerId === userId;
            const url = await getFilePreviewUrl(
              instance,
              account,
              image.id,
              album.id,
              canManage,
            );
            registerUrl(url);
            return [album.id, url] as const;
          } catch {
            return null;
          }
        }),
      );

      if (!active) return;
      setCoverUrls(
        Object.fromEntries(
          entries.filter((entry): entry is readonly [number, string] => entry !== null),
        ),
      );
    };

    void load();

    return () => {
      active = false;
      cleanupRequested = true;
      createdUrls.forEach((url) => URL.revokeObjectURL(url));
    };
  }, [account, albums, instance, isAdmin, userId]);

  return coverUrls;
}

FILE: C:\Users\ShodhanVemulapalli\Downloads\WinCapture\frontend\src\hooks\useFileFavorites.ts
============================================================

import { useCallback, useState } from "react";
import type {
  AccountInfo,
  IPublicClientApplication,
} from "@azure/msal-browser";

import {
  addFavorite,
  removeFavorite,
} from "../api/apiClient";
import type { FileResponse } from "../types/file";

interface UseFileFavoritesOptions {
  instance: IPublicClientApplication;
  account: AccountInfo | null;
  onSuccess: (
    file: FileResponse,
    nextFavorite: boolean,
  ) => void;
  onError: (message: string) => void;
}

export default function useFileFavorites({
  instance,
  account,
  onSuccess,
  onError,
}: UseFileFavoritesOptions) {
  const [pendingIds, setPendingIds] =
    useState<Set<number>>(new Set());

  const setPending = useCallback(
    (fileId: number, pending: boolean) => {
      setPendingIds((previous) => {
        const next = new Set(previous);

        if (pending) {
          next.add(fileId);
        } else {
          next.delete(fileId);
        }

        return next;
      });
    },
    [],
  );

  const toggleFavorite = useCallback(
    async (file: FileResponse) => {
      if (!account || pendingIds.has(file.id)) {
        return;
      }

      const nextFavorite = !file.isFavorite;

      setPending(file.id, true);

      try {
        if (nextFavorite) {
          await addFavorite(
            instance,
            account,
            file.id,
          );
        } else {
          await removeFavorite(
            instance,
            account,
            file.id,
          );
        }

        onSuccess(file, nextFavorite);
      } catch (caughtError) {
        onError(
          caughtError instanceof Error
            ? caughtError.message
            : "Unable to update favorite.",
        );
      } finally {
        setPending(file.id, false);
      }
    },
    [
      account,
      instance,
      onError,
      onSuccess,
      pendingIds,
      setPending,
    ],
  );

  return {
    pendingIds,
    toggleFavorite,
  };
}

FILE: C:\Users\ShodhanVemulapalli\Downloads\WinCapture\frontend\src\hooks\useFilePreviews.ts
============================================================

import { useEffect, useRef, useState } from "react";

import type { FileResponse } from "../types/file";

export type FilePreviewLoader = (
  file: FileResponse,
) => Promise<string>;

interface UseFilePreviewsResult {
  previewUrls: Record<number, string>;
  loading: boolean;
}

export default function useFilePreviews(
  files: FileResponse[],
  loadPreview: FilePreviewLoader,
): UseFilePreviewsResult {
  const [previewUrls, setPreviewUrls] =
    useState<Record<number, string>>({});
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);
  const cache = useRef(new Map<number, { signature: string; url: string }>());
  const pending = useRef(new Map<string, Promise<string>>());
  const currentSignatures = useRef(new Map<number, string>());

  useEffect(() => {
    let active = true;

    const imageFiles = files.filter((file) =>
      file.contentType.startsWith("image/"),
    );

    const signatureFor = (file: FileResponse) =>
      `${file.contentType}:${file.fileSize}:${file.uploadedAt}`;
    currentSignatures.current = new Map(
      imageFiles.map((file) => [file.id, signatureFor(file)]),
    );
    cache.current.forEach((entry, id) => {
      const currentFile = imageFiles.find((file) => file.id === id);
      if (!currentFile || entry.signature !== signatureFor(currentFile)) {
        URL.revokeObjectURL(entry.url);
        cache.current.delete(id);
      }
    });
    setPreviewUrls(Object.fromEntries(imageFiles.flatMap(file => {
      const entry = cache.current.get(file.id);
      return entry?.signature === signatureFor(file) ? [[file.id, entry.url]] : [];
    })));
    setLoading(imageFiles.length > 0);

    if (imageFiles.length === 0) {
      return () => {
        active = false;
      };
    }

    const load = async () => {
      const results = await Promise.all(
        imageFiles.map(async (file) => {
          try {
            const signature = signatureFor(file);
            const pendingKey = `${file.id}:${signature}`;
            let request = pending.current.get(pendingKey);
            if (!request) {
              request = loadPreview(file);
              pending.current.set(pendingKey, request);
            }
            const cached = cache.current.get(file.id);
            const url = cached?.signature === signature ? cached.url : await request;
            const isCurrent =
              active && currentSignatures.current.get(file.id) === signature;
            if (!isCurrent) {
              // Requests shared with a newer effect may still be consumed there.
              if (currentSignatures.current.get(file.id) !== signature) {
                URL.revokeObjectURL(url);
              }
              return null;
            }

            const previous = cache.current.get(file.id);
            if (previous && previous.url !== url && previous.signature !== signature) {
              URL.revokeObjectURL(previous.url);
            }
            cache.current.set(file.id, { signature, url });

            return [file.id, url] as const;
          } catch {
            return null;
          } finally {
            pending.current.delete(`${file.id}:${signatureFor(file)}`);
          }
        }),
      );

      if (!active) {
        return;
      }

      setPreviewUrls(
        Object.fromEntries(
          results.filter(
            (entry): entry is readonly [number, string] =>
              entry !== null,
          ),
        ),
      );
      setLoading(false);
    };

    void load();

    return () => {
      active = false;
    };
  }, [files, loadPreview]);

  useEffect(() => () => {
    currentSignatures.current.clear();
    cache.current.forEach((entry) => URL.revokeObjectURL(entry.url));
    cache.current.clear();
  }, []);

  return {
    previewUrls,
    loading,
  };
}

import { useMemo } from "react";

interface PaginationResult<T> {
  items: T[];
  pageCount: number;
  currentPage: number;
  totalItems: number;
}

export default function usePagination<T>(
  items: T[],
  requestedPage: number,
  pageSize = 10,
): PaginationResult<T> {
  const pageCount = Math.max(1, Math.ceil(items.length / pageSize));
  const currentPage = Math.min(Math.max(1, requestedPage), pageCount);
  const startIndex = (currentPage - 1) * pageSize;
  const pageItems = useMemo(
    () => items.slice(startIndex, startIndex + pageSize),
    [items, pageSize, startIndex],
  );

  return {
    items: pageItems,
    pageCount,
    currentPage,
    totalItems: items.length,
  };
}

import React from "react";
import ReactDOM from "react-dom/client";
import { BrowserRouter } from "react-router-dom";

import { PublicClientApplication } from "@azure/msal-browser";
import { MsalProvider } from "@azure/msal-react";

import App from "./App";
import { AuthProvider } from "./context/AuthContext";
import { appShellClass } from "./components/ui/uiClasses";
import {
  msalConfig,
  validateEnvironment,
} from "./authConfig";

import "./index.css";

const rootElement =
  document.getElementById("root");

if (!rootElement) {
  throw new Error(
    "Could not find the root element.",
  );
}

const root =
  ReactDOM.createRoot(
    rootElement,
  );

function showStartupError(
  error: unknown,
) {
  const message =
    error instanceof Error
      ? error.message
      : String(error);

  root.render(
    <main className={`${appShellClass} min-h-screen px-5 py-8 sm:px-8 sm:py-12`}>
      <section className="mx-auto flex min-h-[calc(100vh-4rem)] w-full max-w-2xl items-center justify-center">
        <div className="surface w-full overflow-hidden rounded-[24px] p-6 sm:p-8">
          <div className="flex items-start gap-4">
            <span className="grid h-11 w-11 shrink-0 place-items-center rounded-[13px] border border-red-200 bg-red-50 text-red-600 shadow-sm">
              <span
                aria-hidden="true"
                className="text-lg font-bold"
              >
                !
              </span>
            </span>

            <div className="min-w-0">
              <p className="text-[10px] font-bold uppercase tracking-[0.14em] text-slate-400">
                WinCapture
              </p>

              <h1 className="mt-2 text-xl font-bold tracking-[-0.025em] text-slate-800 sm:text-2xl">
                Could not start the app
              </h1>

              <p className="mt-3 text-sm leading-6 text-slate-500">
                WinCapture could not complete its
                startup configuration. Check the
                message below and correct the
                environment configuration before
                starting the application again.
              </p>
            </div>
          </div>

          <div className="mt-6 rounded-[15px] border border-red-100 bg-red-50/75 p-4">
            <p className="whitespace-pre-wrap break-words text-sm leading-6 text-red-700">
              {message}
            </p>
          </div>
        </div>
      </section>
    </main>,
  );
}

async function startApp() {
  try {
    const missingEnvironment =
      validateEnvironment();

    if (
      missingEnvironment.length > 0
    ) {
      throw new Error(
        `Missing environment variables: ${missingEnvironment.join(
          ", ",
        )}`,
      );
    }

    const msalInstance =
      new PublicClientApplication(
        msalConfig,
      );

    await msalInstance.initialize();

    root.render(
      <React.StrictMode>
        <BrowserRouter>
          <MsalProvider
            instance={msalInstance}
          >
            <AuthProvider>
              <App />
            </AuthProvider>
          </MsalProvider>
        </BrowserRouter>
      </React.StrictMode>,
    );
  } catch (error) {
    showStartupError(error);
  }
}

void startApp();

import { useEffect, useMemo, useState } from "react";
import {
  AlertCircle,
  FileText,
  FolderOpen,
  Images,
  Search,
  Settings2,
  ShieldCheck,
  Trash2,
} from "lucide-react";
import { useMsal } from "@azure/msal-react";
import { useNavigate } from "react-router-dom";

import {
  getAdminAlbums,
  getAdminFiles,
  getMyAdminAlbums,
  getMyAdminFiles,
  deleteAlbum,
  deleteFile,
} from "../api/apiClient";
import ErrorBanner from "../components/ui/ErrorBanner";
import Pagination from "../components/ui/Pagination";
import { useAuth } from "../context/AuthContext";
import usePagination from "../hooks/usePagination";
import type { AlbumResponse } from "../types/album";
import type { AdminFileResponse } from "../types/file";
import {
  formatDate,
  formatFileSize,
} from "../utils/formatters";

type ContentScope = "mine" | "all";
type ContentType = "albums" | "files";

const PAGE_SIZE = 10;

const scopeButtonClass = (active: boolean) =>
  `min-h-[34px] cursor-pointer rounded-[9px] border-0 px-[11px] text-[9px] font-semibold transition-[color,background,box-shadow] ${active ? "bg-white text-[#1e5f9c] shadow-[0_2px_7px_rgba(42,88,125,0.1)]" : "bg-transparent text-[#71879a] hover:text-[#315c7d]"}`;

export default function AdminPage() {
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const { instance } = useMsal();
  const { account } = useAuth();

  const [albums, setAlbums] = useState<AlbumResponse[]>([]);
  const [files, setFiles] = useState<AdminFileResponse[]>([]);
  const [scope, setScope] = useState<ContentScope>("mine");
  const [contentType, setContentType] = useState<ContentType>("albums");
  const [searchQuery, setSearchQuery] = useState("");
  const [albumsPage, setAlbumsPage] = useState(1);
  const [filesPage, setFilesPage] = useState(1);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    let active = true;

    async function load() {
      if (!account) return;

      try {
        setLoading(true);
        setError(null);
        setAlbums([]);
        setFiles([]);

        const [albumResult, fileResult] = scope === "mine"
          ? await Promise.all([
              getMyAdminAlbums(instance, account),
              getMyAdminFiles(instance, account),
            ])
          : await Promise.all([
              getAdminAlbums(instance, account),
              getAdminFiles(instance, account),
            ]);

        if (!active) return;
        setAlbums(albumResult);
        setFiles(fileResult);
      } catch (caughtError) {
        if (!active) return;
        setError(
          caughtError instanceof Error
            ? caughtError.message
            : "Unable to load admin data.",
        );
      } finally {
        if (active) setLoading(false);
      }
    }

    void load();

    return () => {
      active = false;
    };
  }, [account, instance, scope]);

  const normalizedQuery = searchQuery.trim().toLocaleLowerCase();
  const filteredAlbums = useMemo(
    () => albums.filter((album) =>
      `${album.id} ${album.albumName} ${album.ownerId} ${album.ownerName} ${album.createdAt} ${album.updatedAt ?? ""}`
        .toLocaleLowerCase()
        .includes(normalizedQuery),
    ),
    [albums, normalizedQuery],
  );
  const filteredFiles = useMemo(
    () => files.filter((file) =>
      `${file.id} ${file.originalFileName} ${file.contentType} ${file.uploadedBy} ${file.uploaderName} ${file.albumId ?? ""} ${file.albumName ?? ""}`
        .toLocaleLowerCase()
        .includes(normalizedQuery),
    ),
    [files, normalizedQuery],
  );

  const albumPagination = usePagination(filteredAlbums, albumsPage, PAGE_SIZE);
  const filePagination = usePagination(filteredFiles, filesPage, PAGE_SIZE);

  const changeScope = (nextScope: ContentScope) => {
    setScope(nextScope);
    setAlbumsPage(1);
    setFilesPage(1);
  };

  const changeContentType = (nextType: ContentType) => {
    setContentType(nextType);
    setAlbumsPage(1);
    setFilesPage(1);
  };

  const changeSearch = (value: string) => {
    setSearchQuery(value);
    setAlbumsPage(1);
    setFilesPage(1);
  };

  const handleDeleteAlbum = async (album: AlbumResponse) => {
    if (!account || !window.confirm(`Delete "${album.albumName}" and all its files? This cannot be undone.`)) return;
    try {
      setError(null);
      await deleteAlbum(instance, account, album.id);
      setAlbums((current) => current.filter((item) => item.id !== album.id));
    } catch (caughtError) {
      setError(caughtError instanceof Error ? `${caughtError.message} The album remains listed, so you can retry after resolving the issue.` : "Unable to delete album. You can retry after resolving the issue.");
    }
  };

  const handleDeleteFile = async (file: AdminFileResponse) => {
    if (!account || !window.confirm(`Delete "${file.originalFileName}"? This cannot be undone.`)) return;
    try {
      setError(null);
      await deleteFile(instance, account, file.id);
      setFiles((current) => current.filter((item) => item.id !== file.id));
    } catch (caughtError) {
      setError(caughtError instanceof Error ? `${caughtError.message} The file remains listed, so you can retry after resolving the issue.` : "Unable to delete file. You can retry after resolving the issue.");
    }
  };

  if (loading) {
    return (
      <div className="app-page">
        <section className="skeleton-panel relative flex min-h-[174px] items-center justify-between overflow-hidden rounded-[20px] px-[30px] py-[28px] after:pointer-events-none after:absolute after:inset-0 after:bg-[linear-gradient(120deg,transparent_35%,rgba(255,255,255,0.22)_52%,transparent_67%)] after:content-[''] max-[760px]:[min-height:auto] max-[760px]:px-5 max-[760px]:py-[22px]">
          <div className="skeleton-content">
            <span className="skeleton-line skeleton-line-sm" />
            <span className="skeleton-line skeleton-line-lg" />
            <span className="skeleton-line skeleton-line-md" />
          </div>
          <span className="skeleton-icon skeleton-icon-large" />
        </section>

        <section className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-[17px] max-[960px]:grid-cols-1">
          {[1, 2].map((item) => (
            <div key={item} className="surface admin-list-skeleton">
              <span className="skeleton-line skeleton-line-md" />
              <span className="skeleton-line skeleton-line-sm" />
              <span className="skeleton-line skeleton-line-sm" />
              <span className="skeleton-line skeleton-line-sm" />
            </div>
          ))}
        </section>
      </div>
    );
  }

  return (
    <div className="app-page admin-page">
      <section className="surface relative flex min-h-[174px] items-center justify-between overflow-hidden rounded-[20px] px-[30px] py-[28px] after:pointer-events-none after:absolute after:inset-0 after:bg-[linear-gradient(120deg,transparent_35%,rgba(255,255,255,0.22)_52%,transparent_67%)] after:content-[''] max-[760px]:[min-height:auto] max-[760px]:px-5 max-[760px]:py-[22px]">
        <div className="pointer-events-none absolute right-[-70px] top-[-130px] h-[230px] w-[230px] rounded-full bg-[rgba(119,177,246,0.15)] blur-[2px]" />
        <div className="pointer-events-none absolute bottom-[-155px] right-[26%] h-[190px] w-[190px] rounded-full bg-[rgba(213,230,249,0.35)] blur-[2px]" />

        <div className="relative z-[1]">
          <div className="page-kicker">
            <span className="page-kicker-dot" />
            Administration
          </div>
          <h1 className="page-heading">System overview</h1>
          <p className="page-description">
            Monitor your content or review albums and files across WinCapture.
          </p>
        </div>

        <div className="relative z-[1] grid h-16 w-16 flex-none place-items-center rounded-[18px] border border-[rgba(194,218,237,0.72)] bg-[rgba(255,255,255,0.54)] text-[#397bc7] shadow-[0_12px_26px_rgba(46,101,150,0.075),inset_0_1px_rgba(255,255,255,0.8)] max-[760px]:h-[51px] max-[760px]:w-[51px] max-[760px]:rounded-[15px]">
          <ShieldCheck aria-hidden="true" size={31} strokeWidth={1.45} />
        </div>
      </section>

      {error && (
        <ErrorBanner
          message={error}
          icon={<AlertCircle aria-hidden="true" size={17} strokeWidth={1.8} />}
        />
      )}

      <section className="surface flex flex-wrap items-center gap-3 rounded-[14px] p-[12px] max-[760px]:items-stretch">
        <div className="inline-flex rounded-[11px] border border-[#dce8f0] bg-[rgba(244,248,251,0.8)] p-[3px]" role="group" aria-label="Content scope">
          <button type="button" aria-pressed={scope === "mine"} className={scopeButtonClass(scope === "mine")} onClick={() => changeScope("mine")}>My Content</button>
          <button type="button" aria-pressed={scope === "all"} className={scopeButtonClass(scope === "all")} onClick={() => changeScope("all")}>All Content</button>
        </div>

        <div className="inline-flex rounded-[11px] border border-[#dce8f0] bg-[rgba(244,248,251,0.8)] p-[3px]" role="group" aria-label="Content type">
          <button type="button" aria-pressed={contentType === "albums"} className={scopeButtonClass(contentType === "albums")} onClick={() => changeContentType("albums")}>
            <Images aria-hidden="true" className="mr-[5px] inline-block align-[-3px]" size={13} />Albums
          </button>
          <button type="button" aria-pressed={contentType === "files"} className={scopeButtonClass(contentType === "files")} onClick={() => changeContentType("files")}>
            <FolderOpen aria-hidden="true" className="mr-[5px] inline-block align-[-3px]" size={13} />Files
          </button>
        </div>

        <label className="ml-auto flex min-h-[38px] min-w-[min(260px,100%)] flex-1 items-center gap-2 rounded-[10px] border border-[#dbe8f1] bg-[rgba(255,255,255,0.63)] px-[10px] text-[#8195a5] shadow-[0_5px_14px_rgba(45,94,136,0.03)] focus-within:border-[#8bbdec] max-[760px]:ml-0 max-[760px]:basis-full">
          <Search aria-hidden="true" size={16} />
          <input
            type="search"
            value={searchQuery}
            onChange={(event) => changeSearch(event.target.value)}
            placeholder={`Search ${contentType}...`}
            aria-label={`Search ${contentType}`}
            className="w-full min-w-0 border-0 bg-transparent text-[10px] text-[#24445d] outline-none placeholder:text-[#98aab7]"
          />
        </label>
      </section>

      <section className="surface min-w-0 overflow-hidden rounded-[17px]">
        <header className="flex min-h-[70px] items-center justify-between gap-3 border-b border-[#e4edf4] px-[18px]">
          <div>
            <p className="dashboard-panel-kicker">
              {scope === "mine" ? "My Content" : "All Content"}
            </p>
            <h2 className="mt-1 mb-0 text-[16px] leading-[1.25] font-[650] tracking-[-0.025em] text-[#163855]">
              {contentType === "albums" ? "Albums" : "Files"}
            </h2>
          </div>
          <span className="inline-flex h-[27px] min-w-7 items-center justify-center rounded-full border border-[#dfeaf2] bg-[rgba(255,255,255,0.55)] px-2 text-[9px] font-semibold text-[#688096]">
            {contentType === "albums" ? filteredAlbums.length : filteredFiles.length}
          </span>
        </header>

        {contentType === "albums" ? (
          filteredAlbums.length === 0 ? (
            <div className="flex min-h-[220px] items-center justify-center gap-[11px] p-[22px]">
              <span className="grid h-[42px] w-[42px] flex-none place-items-center rounded-[12px] bg-[#e9f3ff] text-[#2a7bd4]">
                <Images aria-hidden="true" size={23} strokeWidth={1.5} />
              </span>
              <div>
                <strong className="block text-[10px] font-[650] text-[#405a70]">
                  {searchQuery ? "No albums match your search" : "No albums available"}
                </strong>
                <p className="mt-1 text-[8px] text-[#8e9eaa]">
                  {searchQuery ? "Try another album name or owner." : "There are currently no albums in this scope."}
                </p>
              </div>
            </div>
          ) : (
            <div className="flex flex-col">
              {albumPagination.items.map((album) => (
                <div key={album.id} className="flex min-h-[72px] items-center gap-[10px] border-b border-[#e6eef4] px-[15px] py-[10px] transition-[background,padding] duration-[170ms] hover:bg-[rgba(231,242,252,0.58)] hover:pl-[18px] last:border-b-0 max-[520px]:gap-[7px] max-[520px]:px-[11px]">
                  <span className="grid h-[37px] w-[37px] flex-none place-items-center rounded-[11px] bg-[#eaf3ff] text-[#2b7ed8]">
                    <Images aria-hidden="true" size={17} strokeWidth={1.7} />
                  </span>
                  <div className="grid min-w-0 flex-1 gap-1">
                    <strong className="overflow-hidden text-ellipsis whitespace-nowrap text-[10px] font-semibold text-[#355269]" title={album.albumName}>
                      {album.albumName}
                    </strong>
                    <span className="overflow-hidden text-ellipsis whitespace-nowrap text-[8px] text-[#879aa9]">
                      Owner: {album.ownerName} (#{album.ownerId})
                    </span>
                  </div>
                  <div className="grid flex-none gap-0.5 text-right max-[520px]:hidden">
                    <small className="text-[7px] text-[#a0aeb9]">Created</small>
                    <strong className="text-[8px] font-semibold text-[#73899b]">{formatDate(album.createdAt)}</strong>
                  </div>
                  <span className="flex-none text-[8px] text-[#9baab6] max-[520px]:hidden">#{album.id}</span>
                  <div className="flex flex-none items-center gap-1">
                    <button type="button" onClick={() => navigate(`/albums/${album.id}`)} className="inline-flex min-h-[30px] items-center gap-1 rounded-[8px] border border-[#dce8f0] bg-[rgba(255,255,255,0.7)] px-2 text-[9px] font-semibold text-[#397bc7] hover:bg-white" aria-label={`Manage access to ${album.albumName}`} title="Open album and manage access">
                      <Settings2 aria-hidden="true" size={13} />Manage
                    </button>
                    <button type="button" onClick={() => void handleDeleteAlbum(album)} className="grid h-[30px] w-[30px] place-items-center rounded-[8px] border border-[#f0dada] bg-[rgba(255,255,255,0.7)] text-[#ae5555] hover:bg-[#fff1f1]" aria-label={`Delete album ${album.albumName}`} title="Delete album">
                      <Trash2 aria-hidden="true" size={13} />
                    </button>
                  </div>
                </div>
              ))}
            </div>
          )
        ) : filteredFiles.length === 0 ? (
          <div className="flex min-h-[220px] items-center justify-center gap-[11px] p-[22px]">
            <span className="grid h-[42px] w-[42px] flex-none place-items-center rounded-[12px] bg-[#efedff] text-[#6e65ce]">
              <FileText aria-hidden="true" size={23} strokeWidth={1.5} />
            </span>
            <div>
              <strong className="block text-[10px] font-[650] text-[#405a70]">
                {searchQuery ? "No files match your search" : "No files available"}
              </strong>
              <p className="mt-1 text-[8px] text-[#8e9eaa]">
                {searchQuery ? "Try another filename, uploader, or album." : "There are currently no files in this scope."}
              </p>
            </div>
          </div>
        ) : (
          <div className="flex flex-col">
            {filePagination.items.map((file) => (
              <div key={file.id} className="flex min-h-[72px] items-center gap-[10px] border-b border-[#e6eef4] px-[15px] py-[10px] transition-[background,padding] duration-[170ms] hover:bg-[rgba(231,242,252,0.58)] hover:pl-[18px] last:border-b-0 max-[520px]:gap-[7px] max-[520px]:px-[11px]">
                <span className="grid h-[37px] w-[37px] flex-none place-items-center rounded-[11px] bg-[#efedff] text-[#6c68d2]">
                  <FolderOpen aria-hidden="true" size={17} strokeWidth={1.7} />
                </span>
                <div className="grid min-w-0 flex-1 gap-1">
                  <strong className="overflow-hidden text-ellipsis whitespace-nowrap text-[10px] font-semibold text-[#355269]" title={file.originalFileName}>
                    {file.originalFileName}
                  </strong>
                  <span className="overflow-hidden text-ellipsis whitespace-nowrap text-[8px] text-[#879aa9]">
                    Uploaded by: {file.uploaderName} (#{file.uploadedBy})
                    {file.albumId !== null
                      ? ` \u00B7 Album: ${file.albumName ?? "Unknown album"} (#${file.albumId})`
                      : " \u00B7 Personal file"}
                  </span>
                </div>
                <div className="grid flex-none gap-0.5 text-right max-[520px]:hidden">
                  <small className="text-[7px] text-[#a0aeb9]">{file.contentType}</small>
                  <strong className="text-[8px] font-semibold text-[#73899b]">{formatFileSize(file.fileSize)}</strong>
                </div>
                <span className="flex-none text-[8px] text-[#9baab6] max-[520px]:hidden">#{file.id}</span>
                <button type="button" onClick={() => void handleDeleteFile(file)} className="grid h-[30px] w-[30px] flex-none place-items-center rounded-[8px] border border-[#f0dada] bg-[rgba(255,255,255,0.7)] text-[#ae5555] hover:bg-[#fff1f1]" aria-label={`Delete file ${file.originalFileName}`} title="Delete file">
                  <Trash2 aria-hidden="true" size={13} />
                </button>
              </div>
            ))}
          </div>
        )}

        {((contentType === "albums" && filteredAlbums.length > 0) ||
          (contentType === "files" && filteredFiles.length > 0)) && (
          <Pagination
            currentPage={contentType === "albums" ? albumPagination.currentPage : filePagination.currentPage}
            pageCount={contentType === "albums" ? albumPagination.pageCount : filePagination.pageCount}
            onPageChange={contentType === "albums" ? setAlbumsPage : setFilesPage}
          />
        )}
      </section>
    </div>
  );
}
