import {
  useCallback,
  useEffect,
  useRef,
  useState,
  type ChangeEvent,
} from "react";
import {
  ArrowLeft,
  CalendarDays,
  Check,
  ChevronDown,
  Download,
  Edit3,
  Images,
  Link2,
  Save,
  Share2,
  Trash2,
  Upload,
  UserPlus,
  Users,
} from "lucide-react";
import { useMsal } from "@azure/msal-react";
import {
  Link,
  useNavigate,
  useParams,
} from "react-router-dom";

import {
  addAlbumMember,
  deleteAlbum,
  deleteAlbumFile,
  downloadAlbumFile,
  getAlbum,
  getAlbumFile,
  getAlbumFiles,
  getAlbumMembers,
  getFilePreviewUrl,
  removeAlbumMember,
  replaceAlbumFile,
  updateAlbum,
  updateAlbumMember,
  uploadAlbumFile,
} from "../api/apiClient";
import ErrorBanner from "../components/ui/ErrorBanner";
import Pagination from "../components/ui/Pagination";
import FileCardGrid from "../components/files/FileCardGrid";
import FileDetailsDialog from "../components/files/FileDetailsDialog";
import ImagePreviewModal from "../components/files/ImagePreviewModal";
import useFileFavorites from "../hooks/useFileFavorites";
import useFilePreviews from "../hooks/useFilePreviews";
import usePagination from "../hooks/usePagination";
import { useAuth } from "../context/AuthContext";

import type {
  AlbumMemberResponse,
  AlbumResponse,
} from "../types/album";
import type { FileResponse } from "../types/file";
import { formatDate } from "../utils/formatters";

const PAGE_SIZE = 12;

const sharePanelClass =
  "min-w-0 p-5 max-[760px]:p-[17px] max-[520px]:p-[14px]";
const permissionToggleClass =
  "inline-flex h-7 min-w-[52px] cursor-pointer items-center justify-center gap-[5px] rounded-full border border-[#dde7ee] bg-[#f4f7fa] px-2 text-[8px] font-bold text-[#8b9aa6] transition-[color,background,border-color] duration-[180ms] disabled:opacity-[0.56]";
const memberPermissionClass =
  "inline-flex min-h-[26px] cursor-pointer items-center justify-center gap-1 rounded-[7px] border border-[#dce8ef] bg-[#f4f8fa] px-[7px] text-[7px] font-[650] text-[#7c91a2] transition-[color,background,border-color] duration-[160ms] disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:opacity-[0.47]";

export default function AlbumDetailPage() {
  const { instance } = useMsal();
  const navigate = useNavigate();

  const {
    account,
    user,
  } = useAuth();

  const { albumId } =
    useParams();

  const fileInputRef =
    useRef<HTMLInputElement | null>(null);

  const replaceInputRef =
    useRef<HTMLInputElement | null>(null);

  const replaceTargetIdRef =
    useRef<number | null>(null);

  const [album, setAlbum] =
    useState<AlbumResponse | null>(null);

  const [files, setFiles] =
    useState<FileResponse[]>([]);
  const [currentPage, setCurrentPage] =
    useState(1);

  const [fileDetails, setFileDetails] =
    useState<FileResponse | null>(null);
  const [previewFile, setPreviewFile] = useState<FileResponse | null>(null);

  const [members, setMembers] =
    useState<AlbumMemberResponse[]>([]);

  const [loading, setLoading] =
    useState(true);

  const [error, setError] =
    useState<string | null>(null);

  const [uploading, setUploading] =
    useState(false);

  const [deletingAlbum, setDeletingAlbum] =
    useState(false);

  const [memberEmail, setMemberEmail] =
    useState("");

  const [canView, setCanView] =
    useState(true);

  const [canDownload, setCanDownload] =
    useState(true);

  const [albumNameDraft, setAlbumNameDraft] =
    useState("");

  const [editingName, setEditingName] =
    useState(false);

  const numericAlbumId =
    Number(
      albumId ?? "0",
    );

  const canManageAlbum = Boolean(
    album &&
      user &&
      (user.role === "Admin" ||
        album.ownerId === user.userId),
  );

  const loadPreview = useCallback(
    async (file: FileResponse) => {
      if (!account) {
        throw new Error(
          "An authenticated account is required.",
        );
      }

      return getFilePreviewUrl(
        instance,
        account,
        file.id,
        numericAlbumId,
        canManageAlbum,
      );
    },
    [
      account,
      canManageAlbum,
      instance,
      numericAlbumId,
    ],
  );

  const pagination = usePagination(
    files,
    currentPage,
    PAGE_SIZE,
  );
  const {
    previewUrls,
    loading: previewsLoading,
  } = useFilePreviews(pagination.items, loadPreview);
  const albumCoverPreview = files.find(
    (file) =>
      file.contentType.startsWith("image/") &&
      previewUrls[file.id],
  );

  const loadAlbumData = async () => {
    if (
      !account ||
      !Number.isFinite(numericAlbumId)
    ) {
      return;
    }

    try {
      setLoading(true);
      setError(null);

      const albumResult = await getAlbum(
        instance,
        account,
        numericAlbumId,
      );

      setAlbum(albumResult);
      setAlbumNameDraft(
        albumResult.albumName,
      );

      const nextCanManageAlbum =
        user?.role === "Admin" ||
        albumResult.ownerId === user?.userId;

      const fileResult =
        await getAlbumFiles(
          instance,
          account,
          numericAlbumId,
        );

      setFiles(fileResult);
      setCurrentPage(1);

      if (nextCanManageAlbum) {
        try {
          setMembers(
            await getAlbumMembers(
              instance,
              account,
              numericAlbumId,
            ),
          );
        } catch {
          setMembers([]);
          setError(
            "Album opened, but sharing settings could not be loaded.",
          );
        }
      } else {
        setMembers([]);
      }
    } catch (caughtError) {
      setError(
        caughtError instanceof Error
          ? caughtError.message
          : "Unable to load album details.",
      );
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  };

  useEffect(
    () => {
      if (!Number.isSafeInteger(numericAlbumId) || numericAlbumId <= 0) {
        setAlbum(null);
        setFiles([]);
        setMembers([]);
        setError("The album ID is invalid.");
        setLoading(false);
        return;
      }
      void loadAlbumData();
    },
    [
      account,
      instance,
      numericAlbumId,
      user?.role,
      user?.userId,
    ],
  );

  const handleFavoriteSuccess =
    useCallback(
      (
        file: FileResponse,
        nextFavorite: boolean,
      ) => {
        setFiles((previous) =>
          previous.map(
            (currentFile) =>
              currentFile.id === file.id
                ? {
                    ...currentFile,
                    isFavorite:
                      nextFavorite,
                  }
                : currentFile,
          ),
        );
      },
      [],
    );

  const handleFavoriteError =
    useCallback(
      (message: string) => {
        setError(message);
      },
      [],
    );

  const {
    pendingIds:
      favoritePendingIds,
    toggleFavorite,
  } = useFileFavorites({
    instance,
    account,
    onSuccess:
      handleFavoriteSuccess,
    onError:
      handleFavoriteError,
  });

  const handleUpload =
    async (
      event: ChangeEvent<HTMLInputElement>,
    ) => {
      const selectedFile =
        event.target.files?.[0];

      if (
        !selectedFile ||
        !account ||
        !Number.isFinite(
          numericAlbumId,
        )
      ) {
        return;
      }

      try {
        setUploading(true);
        setError(null);

        await uploadAlbumFile(
          instance,
          account,
          numericAlbumId,
          selectedFile,
        );

        event.target.value = "";

        await loadAlbumData();
      } catch (
        caughtError
      ) {
        setError(
          caughtError instanceof Error
            ? caughtError.message
            : "Upload failed.",
        );
      } finally {
        setUploading(false);
      }
    };

  const handleDownload =
    async (
      file: FileResponse,
    ) => {
      if (
        !account ||
        !Number.isFinite(
          numericAlbumId,
        )
      ) {
        return;
      }

      try {
        setError(null);

        await downloadAlbumFile(
          instance,
          account,
          numericAlbumId,
          file.id,
          file.originalFileName,
        );
      } catch (
        caughtError
      ) {
        setError(
          caughtError instanceof Error
            ? caughtError.message
            : "Download failed.",
        );
      }
    };

  const handleDelete =
    async (
      fileId: number,
    ) => {
      if (
        !account ||
        !Number.isFinite(
          numericAlbumId,
        )
      ) {
        return;
      }

      try {
        setError(null);

        await deleteAlbumFile(
          instance,
          account,
          numericAlbumId,
          fileId,
        );

        await loadAlbumData();
      } catch (
        caughtError
      ) {
        setError(
          caughtError instanceof Error
            ? `${caughtError.message} The file remains listed, so you can retry after resolving the issue.`
            : "Delete failed. The file remains listed, so you can retry after resolving the issue.",
        );
      }
    };

  const handleReplace =
    async (
      event: ChangeEvent<HTMLInputElement>,
    ) => {
      const selectedFile =
        event.target.files?.[0];

      const fileId =
        replaceTargetIdRef.current;

      if (
        !selectedFile ||
        !account ||
        !Number.isFinite(
          numericAlbumId,
        ) ||
        fileId === null
      ) {
        return;
      }

      try {
        setError(null);

        await replaceAlbumFile(
          instance,
          account,
          numericAlbumId,
          fileId,
          selectedFile,
        );

        await loadAlbumData();
      } catch (
        caughtError
      ) {
        setError(
          caughtError instanceof Error
            ? caughtError.message
            : "Replace failed.",
        );
      } finally {
        replaceTargetIdRef.current =
          null;

        event.target.value = "";
      }
    };

  const handleUpdateAlbum =
    async () => {
      if (
        !account ||
        !Number.isFinite(
          numericAlbumId,
        ) ||
        !albumNameDraft.trim()
      ) {
        setError(
          "Album name is required.",
        );

        return;
      }

      try {
        setError(null);

        const updated =
          await updateAlbum(
            instance,
            account,
            numericAlbumId,
            {
              albumName:
                albumNameDraft.trim(),
            },
          );

        setAlbum(updated);

        setAlbumNameDraft(
          updated.albumName,
        );

        setEditingName(false);
      } catch (
        caughtError
      ) {
        setError(
          caughtError instanceof Error
            ? caughtError.message
            : "Unable to update album.",
        );
      }
    };

  const handleDeleteAlbum = async () => {
    if (!account || !album || deletingAlbum) {
      return;
    }

    if (!window.confirm(`Delete "${album.albumName}"? This cannot be undone.`)) {
      return;
    }

    try {
      setDeletingAlbum(true);
      setError(null);
      await deleteAlbum(instance, account, album.id);
      navigate("/albums", { replace: true });
    } catch (caughtError) {
      setError(
        caughtError instanceof Error
          ? `${caughtError.message} The album remains listed, so you can retry after resolving the issue.`
          : "Unable to delete album. You can retry after resolving the issue.",
      );
    } finally {
      setDeletingAlbum(false);
    }
  };

  const handleShowFileDetails = async (file: FileResponse) => {
    if (!account) return;

    try {
      setError(null);
      setFileDetails(
        await getAlbumFile(
          instance,
          account,
          numericAlbumId,
          file.id,
        ),
      );
    } catch (caughtError) {
      setError(
        caughtError instanceof Error
          ? caughtError.message
          : "Unable to load file details.",
      );
    }
  };

  const handleAddMember =
    async () => {
      if (
        !account ||
        !Number.isFinite(
          numericAlbumId,
        ) ||
        !memberEmail.trim()
      ) {
        setError(
          "An email address is required to share the album.",
        );

        return;
      }

      try {
        setError(null);

        await addAlbumMember(
          instance,
          account,
          numericAlbumId,
          {
            email:
              memberEmail.trim(),
            canView,
            canDownload,
          },
        );

        setMemberEmail("");
        setCanView(true);
        setCanDownload(true);

        await loadAlbumData();
      } catch (
        caughtError
      ) {
        setError(
          caughtError instanceof Error
            ? caughtError.message
            : "Unable to share album.",
        );
      }
    };

  const handleUpdateMember =
    async (
      userId: number,
      nextView: boolean,
      nextDownload: boolean,
    ) => {
      if (
        !account ||
        !Number.isFinite(
          numericAlbumId,
        )
      ) {
        return;
      }

      try {
        setError(null);

        await updateAlbumMember(
          instance,
          account,
          numericAlbumId,
          userId,
          {
            canView:
              nextView,
            canDownload:
              nextDownload,
          },
        );

        await loadAlbumData();
      } catch (
        caughtError
      ) {
        setError(
          caughtError instanceof Error
            ? caughtError.message
            : "Unable to update member access.",
        );
      }
    };

  const handleRemoveMember =
    async (
      userId: number,
    ) => {
      if (
        !account ||
        !Number.isFinite(
          numericAlbumId,
        )
      ) {
        return;
      }

      try {
        setError(null);

        await removeAlbumMember(
          instance,
          account,
          numericAlbumId,
          userId,
        );

        await loadAlbumData();
      } catch (
        caughtError
      ) {
        setError(
          caughtError instanceof Error
            ? caughtError.message
            : "Unable to revoke access.",
        );
      }
    };

  if (
    loading ||
    previewsLoading
  ) {
    return (
      <div className="app-page">
        <section className="skeleton-panel relative flex items-center gap-[17px] overflow-hidden rounded-[20px] p-[22px] max-[760px]:flex-wrap max-[760px]:p-[18px]">
          <div className="h-[78px] w-[95px] flex-none rounded-2xl bg-[#dce9f2]" />

          <div className="skeleton-content">
            <span className="skeleton-line skeleton-line-sm" />
            <span className="skeleton-line skeleton-line-lg" />
            <span className="skeleton-line skeleton-line-md" />
          </div>
        </section>

        <section className="grid grid-cols-3 gap-[17px]">
          {[1, 2, 3].map(
            (item) => (
              <div
                key={item}
                className="surface h-[315px] rounded-[17px]"
              />
            ),
          )}
        </section>
      </div>
    );
  }

  if (!album) {
    return (
      <div className="app-page">
        <section
          role="alert"
          className="surface album-not-found"
        >
          <span className="album-not-found-icon">
            <Images
              aria-hidden="true"
              size={28}
              strokeWidth={1.5}
            />
          </span>

          <h1>
            Album not found
          </h1>

          <p>
            {error ??
              "The requested album could not be loaded."}
          </p>

          <Link
            to="/albums"
            className="primary-action"
          >
            <ArrowLeft
              aria-hidden="true"
              size={16}
              strokeWidth={1.8}
            />
            Back to albums
          </Link>
        </section>
      </div>
    );
  }

  return (
    <div className="app-page gap-5">
      <div className="flex min-h-[22px]">
        <Link
          to="/albums"
          className="inline-flex items-center gap-[7px] text-[11px] font-semibold text-[#648199] transition-[color,transform] duration-[180ms] hover:-translate-x-px hover:text-[#176fca]"
        >
          <ArrowLeft
            aria-hidden="true"
            size={16}
            strokeWidth={1.8}
          />
          Albums
        </Link>
      </div>

      <section className="surface relative flex items-center gap-[17px] overflow-hidden rounded-[20px] p-[22px] max-[760px]:flex-wrap max-[760px]:p-[18px]">
        <div className="relative grid h-[78px] w-[95px] flex-none place-items-center overflow-hidden rounded-2xl bg-[radial-gradient(circle_at_25%_20%,rgba(255,255,255,0.66),transparent_30%),linear-gradient(145deg,#dff2ff_0%,#b7daf6_49%,#dceefa_100%)] max-[520px]:h-[65px] max-[520px]:w-[75px]">
          {albumCoverPreview ? (
            <img
              src={previewUrls[albumCoverPreview.id]}
              alt=""
              aria-hidden="true"
              className="absolute inset-0 h-full w-full object-cover"
            />
          ) : (
            <>
              <span className="absolute right-[-45px] top-[-68px] h-[115px] w-[115px] rounded-full bg-[rgba(75,158,237,0.24)]" />
              <span className="absolute bottom-[-52px] left-[-36px] h-[90px] w-[90px] rounded-full bg-[rgba(255,255,255,0.36)]" />

              <span className="relative z-[1] grid h-12 w-12 place-items-center rounded-[14px] border border-[rgba(255,255,255,0.72)] bg-[rgba(255,255,255,0.5)] text-[#237edb] shadow-[0_12px_27px_rgba(49,110,169,0.11),inset_0_1px_rgba(255,255,255,0.7)] backdrop-blur-[11px] max-[520px]:h-[39px] max-[520px]:w-[39px] max-[520px]:rounded-xl">
                <Images
                  aria-hidden="true"
                  size={31}
                  strokeWidth={1.45}
                />
              </span>
            </>
          )}
        </div>

        <div className="min-w-0 flex-1 basis-0 max-[760px]:basis-[calc(100%-112px)] max-[520px]:basis-[calc(100%-92px)]">
          <div className="page-kicker">
            <span className="page-kicker-dot" />
            Album collection
          </div>

          {editingName &&
          canManageAlbum ? (
            <div className="mt-[6px] flex max-w-[650px] items-center gap-[7px] max-[760px]:flex-wrap">
              <label
                htmlFor="album-name"
                className="sr-only"
              >
                Album name
              </label>

              <input
                id="album-name"
                value={
                  albumNameDraft
                }
                onChange={(
                  event,
                ) =>
                  setAlbumNameDraft(
                    event.target.value,
                  )
                }
                className="field min-w-0 flex-1 max-[760px]:w-full max-[760px]:basis-full"
                maxLength={200}
                autoFocus
              />

              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() =>
                  void handleUpdateAlbum()
                }
                className="primary-action compact-action"
              >
                <Save
                  aria-hidden="true"
                  size={15}
                  strokeWidth={1.8}
                />
                Save
              </button>

              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => {
                  setAlbumNameDraft(
                    album.albumName,
                  );
                  setEditingName(false);
                }}
                className="secondary-action compact-action"
              >
                Cancel
              </button>
            </div>
          ) : (
            <div className="flex items-center gap-2 max-[520px]:items-start">
              <h1 className="mt-[5px] mb-0 overflow-hidden text-ellipsis whitespace-nowrap text-[24px] leading-[1.18] font-bold tracking-[-0.035em] text-[#123653] max-[760px]:text-[21px] max-[520px]:text-[18px]">
                {album.albumName}
              </h1>

              {canManageAlbum && (
                <button
                  type="button"
                  className="grid h-[31px] w-[31px] flex-none place-items-center rounded-[9px] border border-[#dce9f2] bg-[rgba(255,255,255,0.63)] text-[#628097] transition-[color,background] duration-[180ms] hover:bg-[#eef7ff] hover:text-[#1a70cb] max-[520px]:mt-[3px]"
                  onClick={() =>
                    setEditingName(true)
                  }
                  aria-label="Edit album name"
                  title="Edit album name"
                >
                  <Edit3
                    aria-hidden="true"
                    size={16}
                    strokeWidth={1.8}
                  />
                </button>
              )}
            </div>
          )}

          <p className="mt-[7px] mb-0 text-[11px] text-[#71889c]">
            {files.length}{" "}
            {files.length === 1
              ? "file"
              : "files"}{" "}
          </p>

          <div className="mt-[13px] flex flex-wrap gap-2">
            <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-[5px] rounded-full border border-[#dceaf3] bg-[rgba(255,255,255,0.47)] px-2 py-[5px] text-[8px] font-semibold text-[#638096] max-[520px]:gap-[5px] max-[520px]:text-[7px]">
              <UserPlus
                aria-hidden="true"
                size={14}
                strokeWidth={1.8}
              />
              {album.ownerName}
            </span>

            <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-[5px] rounded-full border border-[#dceaf3] bg-[rgba(255,255,255,0.47)] px-2 py-[5px] text-[8px] font-semibold text-[#638096] max-[520px]:gap-[5px] max-[520px]:text-[7px]">
              <CalendarDays
                aria-hidden="true"
                size={14}
                strokeWidth={1.8}
              />
              {formatDate(
                album.createdAt,
              )}
            </span>

            {album.updatedAt && (
              <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-[5px] rounded-full border border-[#dceaf3] bg-[rgba(255,255,255,0.47)] px-2 py-[5px] text-[8px] font-semibold text-[#638096] max-[520px]:gap-[5px] max-[520px]:text-[7px]">
                Updated{" "}
                {formatDate(
                  album.updatedAt,
                )}
              </span>
            )}
          </div>
        </div>

        {canManageAlbum && (
          <div className="flex flex-none items-center gap-2 self-center max-[760px]:ml-auto max-[520px]:ml-0 max-[520px]:w-full">
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() =>
                fileInputRef.current?.click()
              }
              disabled={uploading || deletingAlbum}
              className="primary-action flex-none justify-center disabled:opacity-60 disabled:cursor-wait max-[520px]:min-w-0 max-[520px]:flex-1"
            >
              <Upload
                aria-hidden="true"
                size={16}
                strokeWidth={1.8}
              />

              <span>
                {uploading
                  ? "Adding..."
                  : "Add files"}
              </span>
            </button>
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => void handleDeleteAlbum()}
              disabled={deletingAlbum || uploading}
              aria-label="Delete album"
              title="Delete album"
              className="inline-flex h-9 flex-none cursor-pointer items-center justify-center gap-[7px] rounded-[10px] border border-[rgba(203,154,157,0.55)] bg-[rgba(255,248,248,0.72)] px-3 text-[10px] font-semibold text-[#ad5960] transition-[background,border-color,color] duration-[180ms] hover:border-[rgba(195,105,113,0.7)] hover:bg-[rgba(255,236,237,0.9)] hover:text-[#a83f49] disabled:cursor-wait disabled:opacity-60 max-[520px]:min-w-0 max-[520px]:flex-1"
            >
              <Trash2 aria-hidden="true" size={15} strokeWidth={1.8} />
              <span>{deletingAlbum ? "Deleting..." : "Delete album"}</span>
            </button>
          </div>
        )}

        <input
          ref={fileInputRef}
          type="file"
          className="hidden"
          onChange={handleUpload}
        />

        <input
          ref={replaceInputRef}
          type="file"
          className="hidden"
          onChange={handleReplace}
        />
      </section>

      {error && (
        <ErrorBanner
          message={error}
        />
      )}

      <section className="min-w-0">
        <div className="mb-[15px] flex items-end justify-between gap-[18px]">
          <div>
            <p className="dashboard-panel-kicker">
              Collection content
            </p>

            <h2 className="mt-1 mb-0 text-[16px] leading-[1.25] font-[650] tracking-[-0.025em] text-[#163855]">
              Files
            </h2>
          </div>
        </div>

        <FileCardGrid
          files={pagination.items}
          previewUrls={previewUrls}
          favoritePendingIds={
            favoritePendingIds
          }
          canManage={
            canManageAlbum
          }
          onFavoriteToggle={(
            file,
          ) =>
            void toggleFavorite(
              file,
            )
          }
          onReplace={(file) => {
            replaceTargetIdRef.current =
              file.id;

            if (
              replaceInputRef.current
            ) {
              replaceInputRef.current.value =
                "";

              replaceInputRef.current.click();
            }
          }}
          onDetails={(file) => void handleShowFileDetails(file)}
          onPreview={setPreviewFile}
          onDownload={(file) =>
            void handleDownload(
              file,
            )
          }
          onDelete={(file) =>
            void handleDelete(
              file.id,
            )
          }
          empty={
            <section className="surface flex min-h-[300px] flex-col items-center justify-center rounded-[18px] px-[22px] py-[38px] text-center">
              <span className="grid h-[62px] w-[62px] place-items-center rounded-[19px] bg-[#e7f3ff] text-[#287dd7] shadow-[inset_0_1px_rgba(255,255,255,0.72),0_7px_18px_rgba(45,111,176,0.06)]">
                <Upload
                  aria-hidden="true"
                  size={25}
                  strokeWidth={1.5}
                />
              </span>

              <h3 className="mt-4 mb-0 text-[15px] font-[650] text-[#29445d]">
                No files in this album
              </h3>

              <p className="mt-[7px] mb-[18px] max-w-[390px] text-[10px] leading-[1.6] text-[#8497a7]">
                Add images or PDF documents
                to start building this
                collection.
              </p>

              {canManageAlbum && (
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() =>
                    fileInputRef.current?.click()
                  }
                  className="primary-action"
                >
                  <Upload
                    aria-hidden="true"
                    size={16}
                    strokeWidth={1.8}
                  />
                  Add your first file
                </button>
              )}
            </section>
          }
        />
        {files.length > 0 && (
          <Pagination
            currentPage={pagination.currentPage}
            pageCount={pagination.pageCount}
            onPageChange={setCurrentPage}
          />
        )}
      </section>

      {canManageAlbum && (
        <details className="surface group overflow-hidden rounded-[18px]">
          <summary className="flex min-h-[76px] cursor-pointer select-none items-center gap-[11px] px-4 py-3 transition-colors duration-[180ms] hover:bg-[rgba(235,244,251,0.5)] group-open:border-b group-open:border-[#e4edf4] max-[520px]:px-3 [&::-webkit-details-marker]:hidden">
            <span className="grid h-[38px] w-[38px] flex-none place-items-center rounded-[11px] bg-[#e8f3ff] text-[#2d7dd7]">
              <Share2
                aria-hidden="true"
                size={18}
                strokeWidth={1.8}
              />
            </span>

            <span className="grid min-w-0 flex-1 gap-[3px]">
              <strong className="text-[11px] font-[650] text-[#35526a]">
                Share access
              </strong>

              <small className="text-[8px] text-[#8799a7]">
                Manage who can view and
                download this album
              </small>
            </span>

            <span className="inline-flex h-[27px] min-w-[34px] items-center justify-center gap-1 rounded-full bg-[#eef4f8] px-2 text-[8px] font-semibold text-[#6d8395]">
              <Users
                aria-hidden="true"
                size={14}
                strokeWidth={1.8}
              />
              {members.length}
            </span>

            <ChevronDown
              aria-hidden="true"
              className="text-[#8da0af] transition-transform duration-[180ms] group-open:rotate-180"
              size={18}
              strokeWidth={1.8}
            />
          </summary>

          <div className="grid grid-cols-[minmax(280px,0.85fr)_minmax(0,1.45fr)] gap-0 max-[960px]:grid-cols-1 max-[760px]:block max-[520px]:overflow-hidden">
            <div className={`${sharePanelClass} border-r border-[#e4edf4] max-[960px]:border-r-0 max-[960px]:border-b max-[960px]:border-[#e4edf4]`}>
              <div className="mb-[15px] flex items-center justify-between gap-[14px]">
                <div>
                  <p className="dashboard-panel-kicker">
                    Album permissions
                  </p>

                  <h3 className="mt-1 mb-0 text-[16px] leading-[1.25] font-[650] tracking-[-0.025em] text-[#163855]">
                    Invite a WinWire user
                  </h3>
                </div>

                <span className="grid h-[33px] w-[33px] flex-none place-items-center rounded-[10px] bg-[#eaf4ff] text-[#347fcf]">
                  <UserPlus
                    aria-hidden="true"
                    size={17}
                    strokeWidth={1.8}
                  />
                </span>
              </div>

              <label
                htmlFor="share-email"
                className="field-label"
              >
                Email address
              </label>

              <input
                id="share-email"
                type="email"
                value={memberEmail}
                onChange={(
                  event,
                ) =>
                  setMemberEmail(
                    event.target.value,
                  )
                }
                placeholder="name@winwire.com"
                className="field w-full"
              />

              <div className="mt-[11px] overflow-hidden rounded-xl border border-[#e4edf3] bg-[rgba(255,255,255,0.28)]">
                <div className="flex min-h-[60px] items-center justify-between gap-3 border-b border-[#e8eef3] px-3 py-[10px] last:border-0">
                  <div className="grid min-w-0 gap-[3px]">
                    <strong className="text-[9px] font-[650] text-[#4b647a]">
                      Can view
                    </strong>

                    <span className="text-[8px] leading-[1.4] text-[#91a1ad]">
                      Allow this person to
                      open the album.
                    </span>
                  </div>

                  <button
                    type="button"
                    aria-pressed={
                      canView
                    }
                    onClick={() => {
                      setCanView(
                        (
                          previous,
                        ) =>
                          !previous,
                      );

                      if (
                        canView
                      ) {
                        setCanDownload(
                          false,
                        );
                      }
                    }}
                    className={`${permissionToggleClass} ${
                      canView
                        ? "border-[#cfece2] bg-[#edf9f5] text-[#23866f]"
                        : ""
                    }`}
                  >
                    <span aria-hidden="true" className={`h-[6px] w-[6px] flex-none rounded-full ${canView ? "bg-[#1db18a]" : "bg-[#a7b5c0]"}`} />

                    {canView
                      ? "On"
                      : "Off"}
                  </button>
                </div>

                <div className="flex min-h-[60px] items-center justify-between gap-3 border-b border-[#e8eef3] px-3 py-[10px] last:border-0">
                  <div className="grid min-w-0 gap-[3px]">
                    <strong className="text-[9px] font-[650] text-[#4b647a]">
                      Can download
                    </strong>

                    <span className="text-[8px] leading-[1.4] text-[#91a1ad]">
                      Allow files to be
                      downloaded.
                    </span>
                  </div>

                  <button
                    type="button"
                    aria-pressed={
                      canDownload
                    }
                    disabled={
                      !canView
                    }
                    onClick={() =>
                      setCanDownload(
                        (
                          previous,
                        ) =>
                          !previous,
                      )
                    }
                    className={`${permissionToggleClass} ${
                      canDownload
                        ? "border-[#cfece2] bg-[#edf9f5] text-[#23866f]"
                        : ""
                    } ${
                      !canView
                        ? "cursor-not-allowed opacity-[0.56]"
                        : ""
                    }`}
                  >
                    <span aria-hidden="true" className={`h-[6px] w-[6px] flex-none rounded-full ${canDownload ? "bg-[#1db18a]" : "bg-[#a7b5c0]"}`} />

                    {canDownload
                      ? "On"
                      : "Off"}
                  </button>
                </div>
              </div>

              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() =>
                  void handleAddMember()
                }
                className="primary-action mt-4 w-full"
              >
                <Link2
                  aria-hidden="true"
                  size={16}
                  strokeWidth={1.8}
                />
                Share album
              </button>
            </div>

            <div className={`${sharePanelClass} min-h-[300px]`}>
              <div className="mb-[15px] flex items-center justify-between gap-[14px]">
                <div>
                  <p className="dashboard-panel-kicker">
                    Current members
                  </p>

                  <h3 className="mt-1 mb-0 text-[16px] leading-[1.25] font-[650] tracking-[-0.025em] text-[#163855]">
                    People with access
                  </h3>
                </div>

                <span className="inline-flex h-[27px] min-w-[29px] items-center justify-center rounded-full bg-[#eff4f7] text-[8px] font-semibold text-[#718698]">
                  {members.length}
                </span>
              </div>

              {members.length === 0 ? (
                <div className="flex min-h-[205px] flex-col items-center justify-center text-center">
                  <span className="grid h-12 w-12 place-items-center rounded-[14px] bg-[#edf3f7] text-[#6e879b]">
                    <Users
                      aria-hidden="true"
                      size={23}
                      strokeWidth={1.5}
                    />
                  </span>

                  <p className="mt-3 mb-0.5 text-[10px] font-semibold text-[#587087]">
                    No one else has
                    access yet.
                  </p>

                  <small className="text-[8px] text-[#95a4af]">
                    Invite a WinWire user
                    using the form.
                  </small>
                </div>
              ) : (
                <div className="overflow-hidden rounded-xl border border-[#e4edf3] bg-[rgba(255,255,255,0.24)] max-[760px]:rounded-[10px]">
                  {members.map(
                    (
                      member,
                    ) => (
                      <div
                        key={
                          member.userId
                        }
                        className="flex min-w-0 items-center gap-[9px] border-b border-[#e7eef3] px-[11px] py-[10px] transition-colors duration-[170ms] hover:bg-[rgba(235,244,251,0.48)] last:border-b-0 max-[760px]:flex-wrap"
                      >
                        <div className="grid h-[31px] w-[31px] flex-none place-items-center rounded-full bg-[#e8f3ff] text-[9px] font-bold text-[#2f73bb]">
                          {member.name
                            ?.charAt(
                              0,
                            )
                            .toUpperCase() ??
                            member.email
                              .charAt(
                                0,
                              )
                              .toUpperCase()}
                        </div>

                        <div className="grid min-w-0 flex-1 gap-0.5">
                          <strong className="overflow-hidden text-ellipsis whitespace-nowrap text-[9px] font-[650] text-[#3b556b]">
                            {member.name ||
                              member.email}
                          </strong>

                          <span className="overflow-hidden text-ellipsis whitespace-nowrap text-[8px] text-[#8899a6]">
                            {
                              member.email
                            }
                          </span>

                          <small className="text-[7px] text-[#a0aeb8]">
                            Granted{" "}
                            {formatDate(
                              member.grantedAt,
                            )}
                          </small>
                        </div>

                        <div className="flex flex-wrap justify-end gap-1 max-[760px]:w-full max-[760px]:justify-start max-[760px]:pl-10 max-[520px]:pl-10">
                          <button
                            type="button"
                            aria-pressed={
                              member.canView
                            }
                            onClick={() =>
                              void handleUpdateMember(
                                member.userId,
                                !member.canView,
                                !member.canView &&
                                member.canDownload
                                  ? false
                                  : member.canDownload,
                              )
                            }
                            className={`${memberPermissionClass} ${
                              member.canView
                                ? "border-[#d2ece5] bg-[#ebf9f5] text-[#227f6b]"
                                : ""
                            }`}
                          >
                            {member.canView && (
                              <Check
                                aria-hidden="true"
                                size={12}
                                strokeWidth={2}
                              />
                            )}
                            View
                          </button>

                          <button
                            type="button"
                            aria-pressed={
                              member.canDownload
                            }
                            disabled={
                              !member.canView
                            }
                            onClick={() =>
                              void handleUpdateMember(
                                member.userId,
                                member.canView,
                                !member.canDownload,
                              )
                            }
                            className={`${memberPermissionClass} ${
                              member.canDownload
                                ? "border-[#d2ece5] bg-[#ebf9f5] text-[#227f6b]"
                                : ""
                            } ${
                              !member.canView
                                ? "cursor-not-allowed opacity-[0.47]"
                                : ""
                            }`}
                          >
                            {member.canDownload && (
                              <Download
                                aria-hidden="true"
                                size={12}
                                strokeWidth={1.9}
                              />
                            )}
                            Download
                          </button>

                          <button
                            type="button"
                            aria-label={`Revoke access for ${member.email}`}
                            title="Revoke access"
                            onClick={() =>
                              void handleRemoveMember(
                                member.userId,
                              )
                            }
                            className="inline-flex min-h-[26px] cursor-pointer items-center justify-center gap-1 rounded-[7px] border border-[#efd6d8] bg-[#fff3f4] px-[7px] text-[7px] font-[650] text-[#d25259] transition-[color,background,border-color] duration-[160ms] hover:bg-[#ffe9eb] hover:text-[#bd3e47] max-[520px]:w-7 max-[520px]:px-0"
                          >
                            <Trash2
                              aria-hidden="true"
                              size={15}
                              strokeWidth={1.8}
                            />
                            <span className="max-[520px]:hidden">
                              Revoke
                            </span>
                          </button>
                        </div>
                      </div>
                    ),
                  )}
                </div>
              )}
            </div>
          </div>
        </details>
      )}

      {!canManageAlbum && (
        <section className="surface flex items-center gap-[11px] rounded-[13px] px-[15px] py-[13px]">
          <span className="grid h-[34px] w-[34px] flex-none place-items-center rounded-[10px] bg-[#eaf3fa] text-[#4c7597]">
            <Users
              aria-hidden="true"
              size={17}
              strokeWidth={1.8}
            />
          </span>

          <div className="min-w-0">
            <strong className="block text-[10px] font-[650] text-[#446078]">
              Shared album
            </strong>

            <p className="mt-[3px] text-[9px] text-[#899baa]">
              You have access to this
              collection based on the
              permissions assigned to your
              account.
            </p>
          </div>
        </section>
      )}
      {fileDetails && (
        <FileDetailsDialog
          file={fileDetails}
          onClose={() => setFileDetails(null)}
        />
      )}
      {previewFile && <ImagePreviewModal file={previewFile} src={previewUrls[previewFile.id]} onClose={() => setPreviewFile(null)} />}
    </div>
  );
}

import { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import {
  CalendarDays,
  FolderOpen,
  Images,
  ArrowUpRight,
} from "lucide-react";
import { useMsal } from "@azure/msal-react";
import { useNavigate } from "react-router-dom";

import { createAlbum, getAlbums } from "../api/apiClient";
import ErrorBanner from "../components/ui/ErrorBanner";
import Pagination from "../components/ui/Pagination";
import {
  pageHeaderClass,
  pageHeaderSkeletonClass,
} from "../components/ui/uiClasses";
import useAlbumCoverPreviews from "../hooks/useAlbumCoverPreviews";
import usePagination from "../hooks/usePagination";
import { useAuth } from "../context/AuthContext";
import type { AlbumResponse } from "../types/album";
import { formatDate } from "../utils/formatters";

const PAGE_SIZE = 9;

export default function AlbumsPage() {
  const { instance } = useMsal();
  const { account, user } = useAuth();
  const navigate = useNavigate();

  const [albums, setAlbums] = useState<AlbumResponse[]>([]);
  const [currentPage, setCurrentPage] = useState(1);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(
    null,
  );
  const [creating, setCreating] = useState(false);
  const [albumName, setAlbumName] = useState("");
  const pagination = usePagination(albums, currentPage, PAGE_SIZE);
  const coverUrls = useAlbumCoverPreviews(
    pagination.items,
    instance,
    account,
    user?.userId,
    user?.role === "Admin",
  );

  const loadAlbums = async () => {
    if (!account) {
      return;
    }

    try {
      setLoading(true);
      setError(null);

      setAlbums(
        await getAlbums(
          instance,
          account,
        ),
      );
    } catch (caughtError) {
      setError(
        caughtError instanceof Error
          ? caughtError.message
          : "Unable to load albums.",
      );
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  };

  useEffect(() => {
    void loadAlbums();
  }, [account, instance]);

  const handleCreateAlbum = async () => {
    if (!account || !albumName.trim()) {
      setError("Album name is required.");
      return;
    }

    try {
      setCreating(true);
      setError(null);

      const created = await createAlbum(
        instance,
        account,
        {
          albumName: albumName.trim(),
        },
      );

      setAlbumName("");

      navigate(
        `/albums/${created.id}`,
      );
    } catch (caughtError) {
      setError(
        caughtError instanceof Error
          ? caughtError.message
          : "Unable to create album.",
      );
    } finally {
      setCreating(false);
    }
  };

  if (loading) {
    return (
      <div className="app-page">
        <section className={pageHeaderSkeletonClass}>
          <div className="skeleton-content">
            <span className="skeleton-line skeleton-line-sm" />
            <span className="skeleton-line skeleton-line-lg" />
            <span className="skeleton-line skeleton-line-md" />
          </div>

          <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
            <span className="skeleton-field" />
            <span className="skeleton-button skeleton-button-wide" />
          </div>
        </section>

        <section className="grid grid-cols-3 gap-[17px] max-[1200px]:grid-cols-2 max-[760px]:grid-cols-2 max-[760px]:gap-3 max-[520px]:grid-cols-1">
          {[1, 2, 3, 4, 5, 6].map(
            (item) => (
              <article
                key={item}
                className="surface min-h-[315px] w-full min-w-0 cursor-pointer overflow-hidden rounded-[18px] p-0 text-left text-inherit transition-[transform,box-shadow,border-color] duration-[220ms] hover:-translate-y-1 hover:border-[rgba(180,209,231,0.75)] hover:shadow-[0_18px_38px_rgba(49,102,148,0.13),inset_0_0_0_1px_rgba(202,224,240,0.3)]"
              >
                <div className="relative h-[188px] w-full overflow-hidden bg-[linear-gradient(145deg,#e3edf5,#d7e5ef)] after:absolute after:top-0 after:bottom-0 after:w-[35%] after:translate-x-[-130%] after:bg-[linear-gradient(90deg,transparent,rgba(255,255,255,0.56),transparent)] after:animate-[skeleton-shimmer_1.55s_infinite_ease-in-out] after:content-['']" />

                <div className="p-4">
                  <span className="skeleton-line skeleton-line-sm" />
                  <span className="skeleton-line skeleton-line-md" />
                  <span className="skeleton-line skeleton-line-xs" />
                </div>
              </article>
            ),
          )}
        </section>
      </div>
    );
  }

  return (
    <div className="app-page">
      <section className={pageHeaderClass}>
        <div className="min-w-0">

            <span className="page-kicker-dot" />
            Your library
          </div>

          </h1>

            Organize your images and files into
            collections that are easy to access
            and share.
          </p>
        </div>

        <div className="flex flex-[0_1_430px] gap-2 max-[960px]:w-full max-[960px]:basis-auto max-[760px]:flex-col">
          <label
            htmlFor="new-album-name"
            className="sr-only"
          >
            New album name
          </label>

          <input
            id="new-album-name"
            value={albumName}
            onChange={(event) =>
              setAlbumName(
                event.target.value,
              )
            }
            onKeyDown={(event) => {
              if (
                event.key === "Enter" &&
                !creating
              ) {
                void handleCreateAlbum();
              }
            }}
            placeholder="New album name"
            className="field min-w-0 flex-1"
            maxLength={200}
          />

          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() =>
              void handleCreateAlbum()
            }
            disabled={creating}
            className="primary-action flex-none disabled:opacity-60 disabled:cursor-wait max-[760px]:w-full"
          >
            <Images
              aria-hidden="true"
              size={17}
              strokeWidth={1.8}
            />

            <span>
              {creating
                ? "Creating..."
                : "New album"}
            </span>
          </button>
        </div>
      </section>

      {error && <ErrorBanner message={error} />}

      <section className="min-w-0">
        {albums.length === 0 ? (
          <section className="surface flex min-h-[300px] flex-col items-center justify-center rounded-[18px] px-[22px] py-[38px] text-center">

import { useCallback, useEffect, useMemo, useState } from "react";
import {
  ArrowRight,
  FolderOpen,
  Images,
} from "lucide-react";
import { useMsal } from "@azure/msal-react";
import { useNavigate } from "react-router-dom";

import {
  getMyAlbums,
  getFilePreviewUrl,
  getFiles,
} from "../api/apiClient";
import ErrorBanner from "../components/ui/ErrorBanner";
import { useAuth } from "../context/AuthContext";
import useAlbumCoverPreviews from "../hooks/useAlbumCoverPreviews";
import useFilePreviews from "../hooks/useFilePreviews";
import type { AlbumResponse } from "../types/album";
import type { FileResponse } from "../types/file";
import {
  formatDate,
  formatFileSize,
  formatFileType,
} from "../utils/formatters";

const dashboardPanelClass =
  "surface min-w-0 overflow-hidden rounded-[18px]";
const dashboardPanelHeaderClass =
  "flex min-h-[73px] items-center justify-between gap-4 border-b border-[#e4edf4] px-[19px] max-[520px]:min-h-[63px] max-[520px]:px-[14px]";
const dashboardListRowClass =
  "flex min-h-[78px] w-full cursor-pointer items-center gap-[11px] border-0 border-b border-[#e6eef4] bg-transparent px-[17px] py-[11px] text-left text-inherit transition-[background,padding] duration-[170ms] hover:bg-[rgba(228,241,252,0.66)] hover:pl-5 last:border-b-0 max-[520px]:min-h-[68px] max-[520px]:px-3 max-[520px]:hover:pl-[14px]";
const dashboardIconClass =
  "grid h-[39px] w-[39px] flex-none place-items-center rounded-[11px] bg-[#e8f3ff] text-[#2c82e4] max-[520px]:h-[34px] max-[520px]:w-[34px]";

export default function Dashboard() {
  const { instance } = useMsal();
  const { account, user } = useAuth();
  const navigate = useNavigate();

  const [albums, setAlbums] = useState<AlbumResponse[]>([]);
  const [files, setFiles] = useState<FileResponse[]>([]);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);

  const recentAlbums = useMemo(() => albums.slice(0, 4), [albums]);
  const recentFiles = useMemo(() => files.slice(0, 4), [files]);
  const albumCoverUrls = useAlbumCoverPreviews(
    recentAlbums,
    instance,
    account,
    user?.userId,
  );
  const loadFilePreview = useCallback(
    (file: FileResponse) => {
      if (!account) {
        throw new Error("An authenticated account is required.");
      }
      return getFilePreviewUrl(instance, account, file.id);
    },
    [account, instance],
  );
  const { previewUrls: recentFilePreviewUrls } = useFilePreviews(
    recentFiles,
    loadFilePreview,
  );

  useEffect(() => {
    let active = true;

    async function load() {
      if (!account) {
        setLoading(false);
        return;
      }

      try {
        setLoading(true);
        setError(null);

        const [albumResult, fileResult] =
          await Promise.all([
            getMyAlbums(instance, account),
            getFiles(instance, account),
          ]);

        if (!active) return;
        setAlbums(albumResult);
        setFiles(fileResult);
      } catch (caughtError) {
        if (active) {
          setError(
            caughtError instanceof Error
              ? caughtError.message
              : "Unable to load dashboard data.",
          );
        }
      } finally {
        if (active) setLoading(false);
      }
    }

    void load();
    return () => {
      active = false;
    };
  }, [account?.homeAccountId, instance]);

  if (loading) {
    return (
      <div className="app-page">
        <section className="skeleton-panel flex min-h-[218px] items-center justify-between p-[30px] max-[960px]:items-start max-[960px]:flex-col max-[760px]:[min-height:auto] max-[760px]:rounded-[17px] max-[760px]:px-5 max-[760px]:py-[23px]">
          <div className="skeleton-content">
            <span className="skeleton-line skeleton-line-sm" />
            <span className="skeleton-line skeleton-line-lg" />
            <span className="skeleton-line skeleton-line-md" />
          </div>

          <div className="flex gap-2">
            <span className="skeleton-button" />
            <span className="skeleton-button skeleton-button-wide" />
          </div>
        </section>

        <section className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-[14px] max-[760px]:gap-[10px]">
          {[1, 2, 3, 4].map((item) => (
            <div
              key={item}
              className="skeleton-panel flex min-h-[118px] items-center gap-[13px] rounded-[17px] p-[18px] max-[760px]:min-h-[102px] max-[760px]:p-[14px] max-[520px]:min-h-[96px]"
            >
              <span className="skeleton-icon" />
              <span className="skeleton-line skeleton-line-sm" />
              <span className="skeleton-line skeleton-line-value" />
            </div>
          ))}
        </section>

        <section className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-[18px] max-[960px]:grid-cols-1 max-[760px]:gap-[13px]">
          <div className="skeleton-panel min-h-[355px] rounded-[18px]" />
          <div className="skeleton-panel min-h-[355px] rounded-[18px]" />
        </section>
      </div>
    );
  }

  return (
    <div className="app-page">
      <section className="surface after:pointer-events-none after:absolute after:inset-0 after:bg-[linear-gradient(120deg,transparent_35%,rgba(255,255,255,0.22)_52%,transparent_67%)] after:content-[''] relative flex min-h-[218px] items-stretch justify-between gap-7 overflow-hidden rounded-[21px] px-8 py-[30px] max-[960px]:items-start max-[960px]:flex-col max-[760px]:[min-height:auto] max-[760px]:rounded-[17px] max-[760px]:px-5 max-[760px]:py-[23px]">
        <div className="pointer-events-none absolute right-[-85px] top-[-130px] h-[280px] w-[280px] rounded-full bg-[rgba(100,182,255,0.16)] blur-[2px]" />
        <div className="pointer-events-none absolute right-[20%] bottom-[-180px] h-[220px] w-[220px] rounded-full bg-[rgba(188,222,255,0.18)] blur-[2px]" />

        <div className="relative z-[1] flex max-w-[740px] flex-col justify-center">
          <div className="mb-2 inline-flex items-center gap-2 text-[9px] font-bold uppercase tracking-[0.11em] text-[#6e8ba4]">
            <span className="h-[6px] w-[6px] rounded-full bg-[#1b86ee] shadow-[0_0_0_4px_rgba(27,134,238,0.08)]" />
            Your workspace
          </div>

          <h1 className="m-0 text-[clamp(27px,3vw,39px)] leading-[1.08] font-bold tracking-[-0.045em] text-[#0b2f51] max-[760px]:text-[27px]">
            Welcome back,{" "}
            <span className="text-[#2278d7]">
              {user?.name ?? "User"}
            </span>
          </h1>

          <p className="mt-3 mb-0 max-w-[620px] text-[12px] leading-[1.7] text-[#6f879c] max-[760px]:text-[10px]">
            Keep your albums organized, manage your
            files, and access your shared collections
            from one place.
          </p>

          <div className="mt-5 flex flex-wrap gap-[9px] max-[760px]:w-full max-[760px]:[&>button]:flex-1">
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => navigate("/albums")}
              className="primary-action"
            >
              <Images
                aria-hidden="true"
                size={17}
                strokeWidth={1.8}
              />
              <span>Browse albums</span>
            </button>

            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => navigate("/files")}
              className="secondary-action"
            >
              <FolderOpen
                aria-hidden="true"
                size={17}
                strokeWidth={1.8}
              />
              <span>Open files</span>
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>

      </section>

      {error && <ErrorBanner message={error} />}

      <section className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-[14px] max-[760px]:gap-[10px]">
        <button
          type="button"
          className="surface group relative flex min-h-[118px] cursor-pointer items-center gap-[13px] rounded-[17px] px-[18px] py-[18px] text-left transition-[transform,border-color,box-shadow] duration-[180ms] hover:-translate-y-0.5 hover:border-[rgba(169,205,231,0.62)] hover:shadow-[0_17px_34px_rgba(51,105,151,0.09),inset_0_0_0_1px_rgba(197,221,238,0.26)] max-[760px]:min-h-[102px] max-[760px]:p-[14px] max-[520px]:min-h-[96px]"
          onClick={() => navigate("/albums")}

          <span className="grid h-[43px] w-[43px] flex-none place-items-center rounded-[13px] bg-[#e5f1ff] text-[#1678ec] max-[760px]:h-[37px] max-[760px]:w-[37px] max-[760px]:rounded-[11px]">

          onClick={() => navigate("/files")}

            strokeWidth={1.8}
          />
        </button>

      </section>

        <section className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-[18px] max-[960px]:grid-cols-1 max-[760px]:gap-[13px]">
        <section className={dashboardPanelClass}>
          <div className={dashboardPanelHeaderClass}>
            <div>
              <p className="dashboard-panel-kicker">
                Collections
              </p>

              <h2 className="mt-1 mb-0 text-[15px] font-[650] tracking-[-0.02em] text-[#153756]">
                Recent albums
              </h2>
            </div>

            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => navigate("/albums")}
              className="inline-flex cursor-pointer items-center gap-[5px] border-0 bg-transparent text-[10px] font-semibold text-[#1572dd] hover:text-[#0b5fbf]"
            >
              View all
              <ArrowRight
                aria-hidden="true"
                size={14}
                strokeWidth={1.8}
              />
            </button>
          </div>

          <div className="flex flex-col">
            {albums.length === 0 ? (
              <div className="flex min-h-[230px] items-center justify-center gap-3 p-6">
                <span className="grid h-[45px] w-[45px] flex-none place-items-center rounded-[13px] bg-[#e8f3ff] text-[#2b7fd9]">
                  <Images
                    aria-hidden="true"
                    size={21}
                    strokeWidth={1.6}
                  />
                </span>

                <div className="min-w-0">
                  <strong className="block text-[11px] font-semibold text-[#334f68]">
                    No albums yet
                  </strong>

                  <p className="mt-[5px] max-w-[280px] text-[9px] leading-[1.5] text-[#8498a8]">
                    Create your first album to
                    start organizing your files.
                  </p>
                </div>

                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => navigate("/albums")}
                  className="secondary-action compact-action"
                >
                  Open albums
                </button>
              </div>
            ) : (
              recentAlbums.map((album) => (
                <button
                  key={album.id}
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() =>
                    navigate(
                      `/albums/${album.id}`,
                    )
                  }
                  className={dashboardListRowClass}
                >
                  <span className={`${dashboardIconClass} relative overflow-hidden bg-[#e8f2ff] text-[#287bd7]`}>
                    {albumCoverUrls[album.id] ? (
                      <img
                        src={albumCoverUrls[album.id]}
                        alt=""
                        aria-hidden="true"
                        className="absolute inset-0 h-full w-full object-cover"
                      />
                    ) : (
                      <Images
                        aria-hidden="true"
                        size={18}
                        strokeWidth={1.7}
                      />
                    )}
                  </span>

                  <span className="grid min-w-0 flex-1 gap-1">
                    <strong className="overflow-hidden text-ellipsis whitespace-nowrap text-[11px] font-semibold text-[#26445f]">
                      {album.albumName}
                    </strong>

                    <small className="overflow-hidden text-ellipsis whitespace-nowrap text-[9px] text-[#7c91a2]">
                      Owner: {album.ownerName}
                    </small>
                  </span>

                  <span className="flex-none text-[9px] text-[#8ca0b0] max-[760px]:hidden">
                    {formatDate(album.createdAt)}
                  </span>

                  <ArrowRight
                    aria-hidden="true"
                    className="flex-none text-[#9cafbd]"
                    size={15}
                    strokeWidth={1.8}
                  />
                </button>
              ))
            )}
          </div>
        </section>

        <section className={dashboardPanelClass}>
          <div className={dashboardPanelHeaderClass}>
            <div>
              <p className="dashboard-panel-kicker">
                Your library
              </p>

              <h2 className="mt-1 mb-0 text-[15px] font-[650] tracking-[-0.02em] text-[#153756]">
                Recent files
              </h2>
            </div>

            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => navigate("/files")}
              className="inline-flex cursor-pointer items-center gap-[5px] border-0 bg-transparent text-[10px] font-semibold text-[#1572dd] hover:text-[#0b5fbf]"
            >
              View all
              <ArrowRight
                aria-hidden="true"
                size={14}
                strokeWidth={1.8}
              />
            </button>
          </div>

          <div className="flex flex-col">
            {files.length === 0 ? (
              <div className="flex min-h-[230px] items-center justify-center gap-3 p-6">
                <span className="grid h-[45px] w-[45px] flex-none place-items-center rounded-[13px] bg-[#eeeaff] text-[#7161da]">
                  <FolderOpen
                    aria-hidden="true"
                    size={21}
                    strokeWidth={1.6}
                  />
                </span>

                <div className="min-w-0">
                  <strong className="block text-[11px] font-semibold text-[#334f68]">
                    No files uploaded yet
                  </strong>

                  <p className="mt-[5px] max-w-[280px] text-[9px] leading-[1.5] text-[#8498a8]">
                    Upload a file to see it
                    appear in your library.
                  </p>
                </div>

                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => navigate("/files")}
                  className="secondary-action compact-action"
                >
                  Open files
                </button>
              </div>
            ) : (
              recentFiles.map((file) => (
                <button
                  key={file.id}
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() =>
                    navigate("/files")
                  }
                  className={dashboardListRowClass}
                >
                  <span className={`${dashboardIconClass} relative overflow-hidden bg-[#efedff] text-[#6b6bd8]`}>
                    {recentFilePreviewUrls[file.id] ? (
                      <img
                        src={recentFilePreviewUrls[file.id]}
                        alt=""
                        aria-hidden="true"
                        className="absolute inset-0 h-full w-full object-cover"
                      />
                    ) : file.contentType.startsWith(
                      "image/",
                    ) ? (
                      <span className="text-[8px] font-extrabold tracking-[0.04em]">
                        IMG
                      </span>
                    ) : (
                      <FolderOpen
                        aria-hidden="true"
                        size={18}
                        strokeWidth={1.7}
                      />
                    )}
                  </span>

                  <span className="grid min-w-0 flex-1 gap-1">
                    <strong className="overflow-hidden text-ellipsis whitespace-nowrap text-[11px] font-semibold text-[#26445f]">
                      {file.originalFileName}
                    </strong>

                    <small className="overflow-hidden text-ellipsis whitespace-nowrap text-[9px] text-[#7c91a2]">
                      {formatFileType(
                        file.contentType,
                      )}
                      <span aria-hidden="true">
                        {" \u00B7 "}
                      </span>
                      {formatFileSize(
                        file.fileSize,
                      )}
                    </small>
                  </span>

                  <span className="flex-none text-[9px] text-[#8ca0b0] max-[760px]:hidden">
                    {new Date(
                      file.uploadedAt,
                    ).toLocaleDateString()}
                  </span>

                  <ArrowRight
                    aria-hidden="true"
                    className="flex-none text-[#9cafbd]"
                    size={15}
                    strokeWidth={1.8}
                  />
                </button>
              ))
            )}
          </div>
        </section>
      </section>
    </div>
  );
}

FILE: C:\Users\ShodhanVemulapalli\Downloads\WinCapture\frontend\src\pages\FilesPage.tsx
